# S8.2 — Non-rectangular typologies Implementation Plan

> **Status:** Done (controller, D-084; pending the owner's reading) · **Date:** 2026-10-02 · **Roadmap:** [S8.2](2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md#s8--typologies-s81s87-d-083-d-084-d-098) · **Decisions:** D-009, D-026, D-041, D-061, D-065, D-076, D-079, D-082, D-083, D-084, D-087, D-088; [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md), [ADR-002](../decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md) (Tasks 2–4), [ADR-013](../decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) (Task 4), [ADR-008](../decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) (Task 7)
>
> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement S8.2 of D-084: plan generators for the three *High-Density Housing* families without holes that are not bars, as ADR-006 specifies them, with Grasshopper components and icons: `HDHM-TYP-007` (*Point Plate*, the first family with a zone that has no outdoor wall), `HDHM-TYP-003` (*Winged Band*, a comb), and `HDHM-TYP-004` (*Open Court*, a U). Every new family validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plan and rotated and moved by two placements. Running the pipeline on these families found three faults on rotated plans that also affect plans released up to `v0.8.0`; they are fixed test-first (D-088): *Semantic Merge* unites each group as tiles, the surface builder tests whether a vertex lies on an edge at the vertices' original positions, and unions of tiles keep the tiles' own vertices. *Perimeter Core*'s `PlanTooNarrow` message states its precondition, that every part of a footprint be wider than twice the perimeter depth (D-087). Every existing plan, floor, building, and snapshot stays unchanged. S8.2 ends with version 0.8.1 and the local tag `v0.8.1`; nothing is pushed in this run (D-084).

**Architecture:** Each family is a folder `Lod.Generators/<Family>/` with a parameter record (a static `Default` holding the ADR-006 defaults), a preset record with one slot per space type it places, and a generator whose `Layout` returns zone rectangles and the footprint, as in S8.1. The wings of the winged band and the open court are bars of stair bays: the internal `StairBayLayout` of S8.1 gains `Wing` (bays along plan x or plan y from any start, with a zone ID prefix), `Fit` (the bay count and adjusted dwelling width that fill a wing's length), and `FitErrors` (the `InvalidParameter` error when the stairs leave no dwelling width); `Bays` calls `Wing` with the same arithmetic, so the S8.1 plans are unchanged. In `Lod.Core`, `SemanticMerge` unites each group with `PolygonOps.UnionTiles` instead of `Union`; `VertexGrid.IsStrictlyInside` and `IsOnSegment` become instance methods that measure the cells' original points against the distance tolerance in metres, and `LayoutSurfaceBuilder` and `UnionTiles` call them on their grid; `UnionTiles` replaces each result vertex by the tile vertex of its grid cell (`VertexGrid.Restore`); `PerimeterCore` appends its precondition to both `PlanTooNarrow` messages. In `Lod.Grasshopper` three components derive from the S8.1 `TypologyGeneratorComponent`; `scripts/make_icons.py` draws three new icons in the Generate green.

**Tech Stack:** C# 12 (`LangVersion` 12.0); .NET SDK ≥ 8 (`global.json` pins 8.0.100 with `rollForward: latestMajor`); `netstandard2.0` core libraries; Clipper2 2.0.0; RhinoCommon and Grasshopper 8.19.25132.1001 (plugin targets `net7.0`); xUnit 2.9.3, xunit.runner.visualstudio 3.1.5, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.14.1 (tests target `net8.0`); Windows PowerShell 5.1 for `scripts/verify.ps1`; Python 3.10 or later with Pillow for `scripts/make_icons.py` (the icons in this plan were drawn with Pillow 12.3.0).

## Global Constraints

- **Gate:** D-087 and D-088 are recorded on `main` (`1794af1`), and this plan is committed to `main` together with the roadmap's S8.2 stage edits (see [Roadmap edits](#roadmap-edits)) before Task 1 starts. ADR-006 and ADR-011, which specify the three families and the minimum wall and window geometry, were accepted in S8.1 (roadmap S8 gate, D-084); S8.2 changes no record or default of ADR-006. S8.2 depends on S8.1, which is tagged `v0.8.0` locally. Before Task 1 the working tree is clean (`git status --short` prints nothing).
- Rhino 8 (8.19 or later) only (D-003); stages exchange Grasshopper objects, not JSON (D-017).
- `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` have no RhinoCommon or Grasshopper reference; their tests run with plain `dotnet test`.
- Nullable reference types on; warnings are errors; `.editorconfig` rules are enforced in the build; every public member of `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` has XML docs (CS1591 is an error).
- Generation is deterministic; outputs have a stable order: the point plate lists the core `ST`, then `U1…U4` counter-clockwise from the south-west; the winged band and the open court list their wings in a fixed order (band, then wings west to east; north, west, east), each wing stairs first, then dwellings, numbered along the wing's axis.
- Numerical tolerances come only from `ToleranceSettings` (ADR-004); S8.2 adds none and changes none. Task 3 changes where the existing `Distance` tolerance is measured (between the original positions of the vertices instead of between their grid cells, D-088); its value is unchanged, and `ToleranceSettings.cs` is not touched. The minimum wall and window geometry stays in `GeometryLimits` (ADR-011), unchanged.
- **Existing outputs do not change.** The twelve existing snapshots (`linear-plan-canonical`, `stack-two-storeys`, the three `simplifier-*`, the three `aggregator-*`, and the four S8.1 canonical snapshots `stair-bay-bar-canonical`, `stair-pair-canonical`, `gallery-bar-canonical`, `terrace-row-canonical`) stay byte-identical, and every existing test keeps passing. The three fixes change results only where they were wrong: rotated plans whose *Semantic Merge* or single-zone aggregation failed (D-088).
- **Never weaken a check (AGENTS.md rules 11, 12).** Each fix is test-first: the new test fails the way the task says on the code before the fix, then passes with the fix. No tolerance is widened, no validation check is relaxed, and no test is weakened. *Perimeter Core* keeps its checks unchanged; only its error message gains the precondition (D-087).
- **Report, never repair (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6).** A wall or window below the ADR-011 minimum keeps its geometry and gets no window, reported as the info `WindowOmitted`; the plan is produced. A parameter value that would remove an essential relationship of a family (fewer than two wings) or a length that is not positive and finite, and stairs that leave no dwelling width in a wing, are the error `InvalidParameter`, every one reported at once. A plan that *Perimeter Core* cannot zone fails with `PlanTooNarrow`; it is never repaired.
- **Typed records per family** (AGENTS.md): each family has its own parameter and preset records; there is no catch-all parameter class and no dictionary of parameters. Space types are only the existing `DwellingUnit` and `Stair`, so the existing default preset components serve every new generator.
- **Source contract (D-082).** No book text, page images, or copied drawings enter BEMGen; ADR-006 and the precedent page paraphrase the families with their stable IDs. The source repository is not opened or modified while this plan is executed; every text the tasks need is given here.
- **No backward compatibility (D-064).** Three components are added with new GUIDs: *Point Plate* `8ac9044a-8fbe-4964-a9de-2ef8be35094a`, *Winged Band* `e285811d-2fdb-4d31-8b02-62c270b609cf`, *Open Court* `223014ea-3c01-4d2b-b8ba-92ebdc4a7327`. No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes.
- Grasshopper components are thin adaptors (AGENTS.md): every rule lives in `Lod.Core` or `Lod.Generators`; the components read inputs, call a generator, and report its diagnostics.
- Research equivalence rules are not changed (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7): the three fixes make existing rules hold on rotated plans, and the documents that state the rules (ADR-002, ADR-013, ADR-008) are updated in the same task as the code.
- No simulation inside the pipeline (D-016). No CI (D-005): `scripts/verify.ps1` passes locally before every merge to `main`.
- Commits: `type(scope): summary` in lower case, no agent, model, or tool attribution and no `Co-Authored-By` trailers (D-001, D-002); author identity per D-050.
- **Pushes (D-084).** Nothing is pushed in this run: slices are rebased onto local `main`, and the stage is tagged `v0.8.1` locally. Task 16 (the push) is not performed in this run; it waits for the owner.
- `.gh` example definitions are made and saved by a person in Rhino 8; agents never write or check them (D-058). S8.2 adds none.
- S8.2 changes these earlier files: `Strategies.cs` (S3), `SimplifierTests.cs` (S3, last changed in S5), `PolygonOps.cs`, `LayoutSurfaceBuilder.cs`, `GeometryTests.cs` (S2, last changed in S4.2), `VertexGrid.cs` (S4.2), `LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests.cs` (S2, last changed in S8.1), `AggregatorTests.cs` (S4, last changed in S5), `StairBayLayout.cs`, `TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1, last changed in S8.1), and `Directory.Build.props` (S0, last changed in S8.1); and the documents ADR-002 (S0, last changed in S8.1), ADR-013 (S4.2, last changed in S5), ADR-008 (S3, last changed in S5), ADR-006 and `docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md` (S8.1), `docs/architecture/pipeline.md` (S4, last changed in S8.1), `docs/research/program-presets.md` and `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` (last changed in S8.1).

## Slice A — `feature/generators-point-plate`

### Task 1: Point plate generator (`HDHM-TYP-007`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/PointPlate/PointPlateGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/PointPlate/PointPlateGeneratorTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/PointPlate/Snapshots/point-plate-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (S2–S8.1): `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (`protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Validate`, `Layout`, `Describe`, `protected static bool IsPositive(double)`), `PlanParameters(double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees)`, `PlanPresets.Slots`, `PresetSlot(string Input, SpaceType SpaceType, ProgramPreset Preset)`, `PlanLayout(Polygon2 Footprint, IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Zones)`, `PlannedZone(ZoneId Id, string Name, SpaceType SpaceType, Polygon2 Footprint)`, `Polygon2.Rectangle`, `Provenance.Format`, `DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter`, `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`; test support `PlanAssertions` (S8.1: `TilesTheFootprint`, `HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall`, `Neighbours`, `OutdoorFacings`), `Snapshot.Match`, `TextReport.Describe`, `ExampleResidentialPresets`.
- Produces: `public sealed record PointPlateParameters(double DwellingDepth, double CoreWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (8, 6, 3, 0); `public sealed record PointPlatePresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets` (slots `DwellingUnit`, `Stair`); `public sealed class PointPlateGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `PointPlateGenerator`: a square plate of side `CoreWidth + 2 · DwellingDepth` with the core `ST` (*Core*, `Stair`) at `[d, d + c]²` and the dwellings `U1…U4` (*Unit 1…4*) in a pinwheel; `InvalidParameter` for each length that is not positive and finite (`{name} must be positive, got {value}.`); provenance keys `CoreWidth`, `DwellingDepth`.

- [x] **Step 1: Create the branch**

```bash
git switch -c feature/generators-point-plate main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/PointPlate/PointPlateGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

19 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plate (zone IDs and names, 22 m × 22 m, the core 36 m² at x, y = 8–14 m, dwellings 112 m², every corner of every zone, no source zones); tiling for three depth and core combinations; every dwelling adjoining the core along a 6 m wall and facing two orientations; the core without an outdoor wall; the window rule (8 windows, no diagnostic); five invalid lengths; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.PointPlate;

public sealed class PointPlateGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly PointPlatePresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly PointPlateGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new PointPlateParameters(DwellingDepth: 8.0, CoreWidth: 6.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), PointPlateParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlateIsFourDwellingsInAPinwheelAroundOneCore()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Core", "Unit 1", "Unit 2", "Unit 3", "Unit 4" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(22.0 * 22.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 36.0, 112.0, 112.0, 112.0, 112.0 }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(8.0, 8.0), new Point2(14.0, 8.0), new Point2(14.0, 14.0), new Point2(8.0, 14.0) }, Outer(plan, "ST"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 0.0), new Point2(14.0, 0.0), new Point2(14.0, 8.0), new Point2(0.0, 8.0) }, Outer(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(14.0, 0.0), new Point2(22.0, 0.0), new Point2(22.0, 14.0), new Point2(14.0, 14.0) }, Outer(plan, "U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(8.0, 14.0), new Point2(22.0, 14.0), new Point2(22.0, 22.0), new Point2(8.0, 22.0) }, Outer(plan, "U3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 8.0), new Point2(8.0, 8.0), new Point2(8.0, 22.0), new Point2(0.0, 22.0) }, Outer(plan, "U4"));
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Stair, plan.Zones[0].SpaceType);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(8.0, 6.0)]
    [InlineData(5.0, 9.5)]
    [InlineData(11.0, 2.5)]
    public void ZonesTileTheSquarePlate(double dwellingDepth, double coreWidth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default with { DwellingDepth = dwellingDepth, CoreWidth = coreWidth });
        double side = coreWidth + (2.0 * dwellingDepth);

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(side * side, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(5, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryDwellingAdjoinsTheCoreAndHasTwoFacades()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST"));
        Assert.All(new[] { "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4" }, u => Assert.Contains("ST", PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, u)));
        Assert.All(new[] { "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4" }, u => Assert.Equal(6.0, plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "ST" && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == u).Length, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U4"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheCoreHasNoOutdoorWall()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Empty(PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "ST"));
        Assert.Equal(4, plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "ST" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(8, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 6.0, "DwellingDepth")]
    [InlineData(-8.0, 6.0, "DwellingDepth")]
    [InlineData(8.0, 0.0, "CoreWidth")]
    [InlineData(8.0, double.NaN, "CoreWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 6.0, "DwellingDepth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double dwellingDepth, double coreWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new PointPlateParameters(dwellingDepth, coreWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new PointPlateParameters(0.0, -6.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(4, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, new PointPlatePresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(PointPlateGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "CoreWidth=6", "DwellingDepth=8", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default)), "point-plate-canonical");
    }

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(PointPlateParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/PointPlate/PointPlateGenerator.cs` (full file)**

The plate is one footprint rectangle; the four dwelling rectangles meet the core and each other at T-junctions, which the layout surface builder splits into the core's four interzone walls.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.PointPlate;

/// <summary>Parameters of the point-access plate (HDHM-TYP-007, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingDepth">Depth of each dwelling from its façade to the core, m; also the plate's margin around the core.</param>
/// <param name="CoreWidth">Side of the square core, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record PointPlateParameters(
    double DwellingDepth,
    double CoreWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 8 m deep dwellings around a 6 m core (a 22 m square plate), 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static PointPlateParameters Default { get; } = new(8.0, 6.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the point-access plate, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the four dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the core; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record PointPlatePresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Compact point-access tower plate (HDHM-TYP-007, ADR-006): a square plate (R001, aspect ratio 1 instead of a bar) with one central core, a
/// <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/> zone without outdoor walls, around which four dwellings lie in a pinwheel, each adjoining the core along one of its
/// sides and having two façades (R002: every dwelling's access is the one core). The pinwheel fixes the unit count at four; the source supports no
/// count. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>ST</c> (the core), then <c>U1…U4</c> counter-clockwise from the south-west. With <c>d</c> the dwelling depth, <c>c</c> the core
/// width, and <c>s = c + 2d</c>: <c>U1</c> <c>[0, d + c] × [0, d]</c>, <c>U2</c> <c>[d + c, s] × [0, d + c]</c>, <c>U3</c> <c>[d, s] × [d + c, s]</c>,
/// <c>U4</c> <c>[0, d] × [d, s]</c>, and the core <c>[d, d + c]²</c>.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class PointPlateGenerator : PlanGenerator<PointPlateParameters, PointPlatePresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public PointPlateGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(PointPlateGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(PointPlateParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(PointPlateParameters.DwellingDepth), parameters.DwellingDepth),
            (nameof(PointPlateParameters.CoreWidth), parameters.CoreWidth),
        };
        return values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToArray();
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(PointPlateParameters p)
    {
        double d = p.DwellingDepth;
        double inner = d + p.CoreWidth;
        double side = inner + d;
        var zones = new[]
        {
            new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("ST"), "Core", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(d, d, inner, inner)),
            Unit(1, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, inner, d)),
            Unit(2, Polygon2.Rectangle(inner, 0.0, side, inner)),
            Unit(3, Polygon2.Rectangle(d, inner, side, side)),
            Unit(4, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, d, d, side)),
        };
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, side, side), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(PointPlateParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.DwellingDepth), Provenance.Format(p.DwellingDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CoreWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CoreWidth)),
    };

    private static PlannedZone Unit(int number, Polygon2 footprint) =>
        new(new ZoneId($"U{number}"), $"Unit {number}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, footprint);
}
```

## Slice B — `fix/geometry-rotated-plans`

The three tasks of this slice repair rotated plans (D-088). Each starts from a test that fails on the code before its fix, with the message given; the fix makes it pass without changing any existing snapshot. The faults are older than S8.2: on `v0.8.0` the linear plan and the S8.1 gallery bar already fail *Semantic Merge* at some rotated placements (see [Notes for the reviewer](#notes-for-the-reviewer), note 4).

### Task 2: *Semantic Merge* unites its groups as tiles

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (S3 file), `docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md` (S0 document, last changed in S8.1)
- Test: modify `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/SimplifierTests.cs` (S3 file, last changed in S5)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PolygonOps.UnionTiles(IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> tiles) : IReadOnlyList<Polygon2>` (S4.2, D-076: vertices in one grid cell unified, T-junctions inserted, then a union); `PlanTransform.Apply` (S4.2); `PointPlateGenerator` (Task 1); `SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings).Simplify(IGeneratedPlan) : Result<IFloor>`; `DiagnosticCodes.DisconnectedGroup` (S3).
- Produces: `SemanticMerge` unites each connected group of zones of one space type with `UnionTiles` instead of `Union`; a merged zone may have holes (the point plate's dwellings around the core). The check that a group gives exactly one polygon (`DisconnectedGroup`) is unchanged.

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing test — replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/SimplifierTests.cs` (full file)**

One new test, `SemanticMergeUnitesZonesMeetingAtTJunctionsOnARotatedPlan`: the default point plate rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m merges into `Stair-1` and `DwellingUnit-1`, a ring with one hole whose area is the four dwellings' 448 m². The usings `Lod.Core.Plans` and `Lod.Generators.PointPlate` are added; every other test is unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SimplifierTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsConnectedZonesOfTheSameType()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "Corridor-1", "DwellingUnit-1", "DwellingUnit-2" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Zone south = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1");
        Assert.Equal(160.0, south.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "US1", "US2" }, south.SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value));
        Assert.Equal(5.0, south.Program.Find(LoadType.Lighting, LoadBasis.PerFloorArea)!.Value, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergedZonesLoseTheirSharedPartition()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces, s => s.Zone == s.AdjacentZone);
        Assert.Equal(2, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "DwellingUnit-1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesZonesMeetingAtTJunctionsOnARotatedPlan()
    {
        // The point plate's dwellings meet each other and the core at T-junctions; rotated off the axes, a plain union of their footprints leaves
        // slits where clipping-grid rounding moves a T-junction off its edge, so the zones are united as tiles (ADR-013).
        IGeneratedPlan plate = new PointPlateGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, new PointPlatePresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(plate, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Result<IFloor> floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "DwellingUnit-1" }, floor.Value.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Polygon2 ring = floor.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(4 * 112.0, ring.Area), $"Area {ring.Area}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreHasFourPerimeterZonesAndACore()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "CORE" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)) * (18.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)), floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterSouthHasOneCentredWindowWithTheSouthFacadeGlazing()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan()).Value;

        WallSurface south = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "P-South" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Window window = Assert.Single(south.Windows);
        Assert.Equal(1.2 + 9.0 + 9.0, window.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 9);
        Assert.Equal((3.0 - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 9);
        Assert.Equal(window.Width / 24.0, window.Height / 3.0, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterDepthTooLargeIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 10.0).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0)]
    [InlineData(-1.0)]
    public void NonPositivePerimeterDepthIsAnError(double depth)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, depth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet()
    {
        Polygon2 courtyard = Polygon2.Create(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(30, 0), new Point2(30, 30), new Point2(0, 30) },
            new[] { new[] { new Point2(10, 10), new Point2(20, 10), new Point2(20, 20), new Point2(10, 20) } }).Value;
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(courtyard, 3.0, 0.0, new Zone[0], new Surface[0], Provenance.Of("Test", new KeyValuePair<string, string>[0]));

        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorHasOnlyOutdoorWalls()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(floor.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Mixed, zone.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(6, zone.SourceZones.Count);
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(4, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SetpointsAreFloorAreaWeightedAndRecorded()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Thermostat thermostat = floor.Zones[0].Program.Thermostat!;
        Assert.Equal(21.0, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint[0], 9);
        Assert.Equal(Lod.Core.Aggregation.AggregationMethod.FloorAreaWeighted, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation!.Method);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(new ZoneId("ST"), thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources);
        Assert.Equal(5, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 1)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 2)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 1)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 2)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 4)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 1)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 2)]
    public void EverySimplifierSatisfiesTheInvariantsForRandomPlans(string simplifier, int seed)
    {
        LinearPlanParameters parameters = RandomParameters(new Random(seed));

        IFloor floor = Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan(parameters)).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergingTheUnconditionedStairEnlargesConditionedAreaAndIsReported()
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances)));

        CheckResult conditioned = report.Checks.Single(c => c.Check == "ConditionedFloorArea");
        Assert.False(conditioned.Passed);
        Assert.False(conditioned.Enforced);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAnUncoveredFacade()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Surface[] withoutSouthWall = floor.Surfaces.Where(s => !(s is WallSurface w && w.Start.Y == 0.0 && w.End.Y == 0.0)).ToArray();
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, floor.Zones, withoutSouthWall, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "FacadeCoverage" && !c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.False(report.Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NoSimplificationPassesValidation()
    {
        Assert.True(new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.DetailedFloor()).Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAChangedProgram()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Zone zone = floor.Zones[0];
        var tampered = new Zone(zone.Id, zone.Name, zone.SpaceType, zone.Parts, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, zone.SourceZones);
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, new[] { tampered }, floor.Surfaces, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.False(report.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Installed.Lighting" && !c.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Traceability" && !c.Passed);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void SimplifiersAreDeterministic(string simplifier)
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void CanonicalSnapshots(string simplifier)
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), $"simplifier-{simplifier}");
    }

    private static IPlanSimplifier Simplifier(string name) => name switch
    {
        "SemanticMerge" => new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        "PerimeterCore" => new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth),
        "SingleZonePerFloor" => new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(name), name, "Unknown simplifier."),
    };

    private static LinearPlanParameters RandomParameters(Random random) => new(
        Length: 18.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 42.0),
        UnitDepth: 6.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 4.0),
        CorridorWidth: 1.5 + (random.NextDouble() * 1.5),
        TargetUnitWidth: 5.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 7.0),
        StairLength: 3.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 3.0),
        FloorHeight: 2.8 + (random.NextDouble() * 0.7),
        OrientationDegrees: random.NextDouble() * 360.0);
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (full file)**

Only the union of `SemanticMerge` and its summary change; `PerimeterCore` and `SingleZonePerFloor` are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Simplification;

/// <summary>
/// Z1: merges zones of the same space type that are connected through shared walls. Each connected group becomes one zone, the union of its
/// footprints as tiles (<see cref="PolygonOps.UnionTiles"/>), so zones meeting at T-junctions unite exactly on rotated plans; the union may have
/// holes, where the group surrounds zones of another type.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SemanticMerge : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SemanticMerge);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        Dictionary<ZoneId, Zone> byId = plan.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id);
        ILookup<ZoneId, ZoneId> neighbours = plan.Surfaces
            .Where(s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .ToLookup(s => s.Zone, s => s.AdjacentZone!.Value);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var visited = new HashSet<ZoneId>();
        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        var counters = new Dictionary<SpaceType, int>();
        foreach (Zone seed in plan.Zones.Where(z => !visited.Contains(z.Id)))
        {
            var group = new List<Zone>();
            var queue = new Queue<Zone>(new[] { seed });
            visited.Add(seed.Id);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                Zone current = queue.Dequeue();
                group.Add(current);
                foreach (ZoneId next in neighbours[current.Id].Where(n => !visited.Contains(n) && byId[n].SpaceType == seed.SpaceType))
                {
                    visited.Add(next);
                    queue.Enqueue(byId[next]);
                }
            }

            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> union = ops.UnionTiles(group.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint).ToArray());
            if (union.Count != 1)
            {
                return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.DisconnectedGroup,
                    $"Merged group {string.Join(",", group.Select(z => z.Id.Value))} is not one polygon.",
                    seed.Id.Value));
            }

            int number = counters.TryGetValue(seed.SpaceType, out int n) ? n + 1 : 1;
            counters[seed.SpaceType] = number;
            targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{seed.SpaceType}-{number}"), $"{seed.SpaceType} {number}", seed.SpaceType, union[0]));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}

/// <summary>
/// Z2: perimeter zones of a configurable depth plus a core (ADR-008). Each footprint edge owns the trapezoid between the edge and its inward
/// mitred offset, so corners are split along the bisectors; trapezoids in the same orientation bin are merged. Footprints with holes are not supported yet.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PerimeterCore : PlanSimplifier
{
    private readonly double _depth;

    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="perimeterDepth">Perimeter zone depth, m.</param>
    public PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings tolerances, double perimeterDepth)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
        _depth = perimeterDepth;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(PerimeterCore);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        if (!(_depth > 0) || double.IsInfinity(_depth))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Perimeter depth must be positive, got {_depth}.");
        }

        if (plan.Footprint.Holes.Count > 0)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported, "Perimeter/core of footprints with holes is not supported yet (S8, typologies).");
        }

        IReadOnlyList<Point2> v = plan.Footprint.Outer;
        int n = v.Count;
        Point2[] offset = Enumerable.Range(0, n).Select(i => Mitre(v[(i + n - 1) % n], v[i], v[(i + 1) % n])).ToArray();
        Result<Polygon2> core = Polygon2.Create(offset);
        bool keepsDirections = Enumerable.Range(0, n).All(i =>
        {
            Point2 a = v[i];
            Point2 b = v[(i + 1) % n];
            Point2 c = offset[i];
            Point2 d = offset[(i + 1) % n];
            return ((b.X - a.X) * (d.X - c.X)) + ((b.Y - a.Y) * (d.Y - c.Y)) > 0;
        });
        if (!core.IsSuccess || !keepsDirections || Polygon2.SignedArea(offset) <= 0)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core for this footprint.");
        }

        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var trapezoids = new List<(OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            Polygon2 shape = Polygon2.Create(new[] { v[i], v[(i + 1) % n], offset[(i + 1) % n], offset[i] }).Value;
            trapezoids.Add((Orientation.Bin(Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(v[i], v[(i + 1) % n]) + plan.OrientationDegrees), shape));
        }

        double tiled = trapezoids.Sum(t => t.Shape.Area) + core.Value.Area;
        if (!Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, tiled))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter zones and core cover {tiled:0.###} m² of a {plan.Footprint.Area:0.###} m² footprint.");
        }

        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        foreach (IGrouping<OrientationBin, (OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)> bin in trapezoids.GroupBy(t => t.Bin).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
        {
            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> merged = ops.Union(bin.Select(t => t.Shape));
            for (int k = 0; k < merged.Count; k++)
            {
                string suffix = merged.Count == 1 ? string.Empty : $"-{k + 1}";
                targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"P-{bin.Key}{suffix}"), $"Perimeter {bin.Key}{suffix}", SpaceType.Mixed, merged[k]));
            }
        }

        targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId("CORE"), "Core", SpaceType.Mixed, core.Value));
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() =>
        new[] { new KeyValuePair<string, string>("PerimeterDepth", Provenance.Format(_depth)) };

    private Point2 Mitre(Point2 previous, Point2 vertex, Point2 next)
    {
        (double X, double Y) a = InwardNormal(previous, vertex);
        (double X, double Y) b = InwardNormal(vertex, next);
        double scale = _depth / (1.0 + (a.X * b.X) + (a.Y * b.Y));
        return new Point2(vertex.X + ((a.X + b.X) * scale), vertex.Y + ((a.Y + b.Y) * scale));
    }

    private static (double X, double Y) InwardNormal(Point2 from, Point2 to)
    {
        double length = from.DistanceTo(to);
        return (-(to.Y - from.Y) / length, (to.X - from.X) / length);
    }

    private static Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> Fail(string code, string message) =>
        Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(code, message));
}

/// <summary>Z3: the whole floor as one zone.</summary>
public sealed class SingleZonePerFloor : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SingleZonePerFloor(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SingleZonePerFloor);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        SpaceType[] types = plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct().ToArray();
        SpaceType type = types.Length == 1 ? types[0] : SpaceType.Mixed;
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(new[] { new TargetZone(new ZoneId("FLOOR"), "Floor", type, plan.Footprint) });
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}
```

Make these replacements, in order; each "Replace" text occurs exactly once in the file when it is applied. The first is the status line; the second adds the paragraph *Unions of zones (S8.2)* after *Edge matching*.

1. Replace

```markdown
Status: Accepted; superseded in part by [ADR-012](ADR-012-centred-windows.md) (windows through simplification); placement rule refined by [ADR-011](ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md) (minimum wall and window)
```

   with

```markdown
Status: Accepted; superseded in part by [ADR-012](ADR-012-centred-windows.md) (windows through simplification); placement rule refined by [ADR-011](ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md) (minimum wall and window); unions of zones refined in S8.2
```

2. Replace

```markdown
**Edge matching.** Building the surfaces of a zoned plan snaps every vertex to the `Distance` grid, splits zone edges at other zones' vertices, pairs reversed edges of two zones as interzone walls, and marks edges on the plan boundary as outdoors. Any other edge indicates a gap or an overlap and is an error; geometry is never repaired silently (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6). Consecutive collinear segments with the same boundary merge into one wall.
```

   with

```markdown
**Edge matching.** Building the surfaces of a zoned plan snaps every vertex to the `Distance` grid, splits zone edges at other zones' vertices, pairs reversed edges of two zones as interzone walls, and marks edges on the plan boundary as outdoors. Any other edge indicates a gap or an overlap and is an error; geometry is never repaired silently (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6). Consecutive collinear segments with the same boundary merge into one wall.

**Unions of zones (S8.2).** Wherever zones are united into one zone, their footprints are united as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)): the groups of *Semantic Merge* (Z1) as well as the storey outlines of the single-zone floor aggregators. A plain union of footprints that meet at T-junctions, such as the pinwheel dwellings of the point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), splits the group or leaves slits once the plan is rotated off the axes, because rounding on the clipping grid moves the T-junction off its edge. A united zone may have holes, where it surrounds zones of another type (the point plate's dwellings around the core).
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Layout;

public sealed class LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests
{
    private readonly LayoutSurfaceBuilder _builder = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneHasFourOutdoorWallsAndUnresolvedFloorAndCeiling()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] walls = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, walls.Length);
        Assert.All(walls, w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(30.0 * 3.0, walls.Sum(w => w.Area), 9);
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { HorizontalKind.Floor, HorizontalKind.Ceiling }, horizontal.Select(h => h.Kind));
        Assert.All(horizontal, h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, h.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(3.0, horizontal[1].Elevation);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SharedEdgeBecomesAPairOfInterzoneWalls()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface ab = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "A" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
        WallSurface ba = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "B" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
        Assert.Equal("B", ab.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value);
        Assert.Equal("A", ba.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value);
        Assert.Equal(ab.Start, ba.End);
        Assert.Equal(ab.End, ba.Start);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EdgesAreSplitWhereAnotherZoneTouchesThem()
    {
        // A long zone above two short ones: its south edge is shared with both.
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 10),
            Zone("TOP", 0, 5, 20, 10),
            Zone("L", 0, 0, 12, 5),
            Zone("R", 12, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] shared = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "TOP" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(2, shared.Length);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 12.0, 8.0 }.OrderBy(x => x), shared.Select(w => w.Length).OrderBy(x => x));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CollinearPiecesOfOneEdgeMergeIntoOneWall()
    {
        // The plan boundary has an extra vertex halfway along the north edge, which splits the zone's edge; the pieces merge back.
        Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(20, 0), new Point2(20, 5), new Point2(10, 5), new Point2(0, 5) }).Value;

        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(footprint, Zone("A", 0, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] walls = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, walls.Length);
        Assert.Contains(walls, w => w.Start == new Point2(20, 5) && w.End == new Point2(0, 5));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GapsAreErrors()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 9, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OverlapsAreErrors()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 0, 0, 10, 5));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.OverlappingZones);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WindowsAreSizedByEachZonesRatio()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio(
            Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5)).Value,
            id => id.Value == "A" ? 0.5 : 0.1,
            GeometryLimits.Default,
            ToleranceSettings.Default).Value;

        WallSurface[] outdoor = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Zone.Value == "A" ? 0.5 : 0.1) * w.Area, w.GlazedArea, 9));
        Assert.All(surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone), w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RotatedTJunctionsOnTheBoundaryAndBetweenZonesAreRecognised()
    {
        // A pinwheel of four 8 m deep zones around a 6 m core, rotated by 131.4° and moved: the zone corners at (14, 0), (22, 14), (8, 22), and
        // (0, 8) lie on the boundary between its corners, and snapped to the distance grid the one at (8, 22) lies 1.10 grid units off the snapped
        // boundary edge although it is on the exact edge; the edge test measures the original points against the distance tolerance.
        var motion = new PlanMotion(131.4, 15.08, -153.9);
        LayoutZone Moved(string id, double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1) => new(new ZoneId(id), Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, y0, x1, y1).Transform(motion));

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = _builder.Build(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 22, 22).Transform(motion),
            new[] { Moved("ST", 8, 8, 14, 14), Moved("U1", 0, 0, 14, 8), Moved("U2", 14, 0, 22, 14), Moved("U3", 8, 14, 22, 22), Moved("U4", 0, 8, 8, 22) },
            3.0);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        WallSurface[] walls = result.Value.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(8, walls.Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
        Assert.Equal(22.0 * 4 * 3.0, walls.Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).Sum(w => w.Area), 6);
        Assert.Equal(16, walls.Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone));
    }

    private static LayoutZone Zone(string id, double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1) => new(new ZoneId(id), Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, y0, x1, y1));

    private Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> Build(Polygon2 footprint, params LayoutZone[] zones) => _builder.Build(footprint, zones, 3.0);
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Geometry/VertexGrid.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>A cell of the <see cref="VertexGrid"/>, in grid units.</summary>
/// <param name="X">X index.</param>
/// <param name="Y">Y index.</param>
internal readonly record struct GridPoint(long X, long Y);

/// <summary>
/// Snaps points to the distance grid, remembers the first original point of each grid cell, and tests whether a grid point lies on a segment
/// within the distance tolerance. Shared by the layout surface builder and the union of tiles, so both recognise the same vertices and T-junctions.
/// The segment tests measure the cells' original points, not the snapped cells: snapping a point and both ends of a segment it lies on moves the
/// point up to √2 grid units off the snapped segment, so on rotated plans a T-junction exactly on an edge could fail a test on snapped cells.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class VertexGrid
{
    private readonly double _cell;
    private readonly Dictionary<GridPoint, Point2> _originals = new();

    /// <summary>Creates an empty grid.</summary>
    /// <param name="cell">Grid spacing, m: the distance tolerance.</param>
    public VertexGrid(double cell)
    {
        _cell = cell;
    }

    /// <summary>Registered cells, in registration order.</summary>
    public IEnumerable<GridPoint> Keys => _originals.Keys;

    /// <summary>The cell of a point.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The cell.</returns>
    public GridPoint Key(Point2 p) => new((long)Math.Round(p.X / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero), (long)Math.Round(p.Y / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));

    /// <summary>Registers a point; the first point registered in a cell stands for the cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    public void Register(Point2 p)
    {
        GridPoint key = Key(p);
        if (!_originals.ContainsKey(key))
        {
            _originals.Add(key, p);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The first point registered in a cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="key">The cell.</param>
    /// <returns>The point.</returns>
    public Point2 Original(GridPoint key) => _originals[key];

    /// <summary>Position of <paramref name="p"/> along <paramref name="a"/>–<paramref name="b"/>, 0 at <paramref name="a"/> and 1 at <paramref name="b"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The parameter.</returns>
    public static double Parameter(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X;
        double dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        return (((p.X - a.X) * dx) + ((p.Y - a.Y) * dy)) / ((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Within the distance tolerance of the line and strictly between the end points (by more than half the tolerance), measured between the
    /// original points of the cells.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> splits the segment.</returns>
    public bool IsStrictlyInside(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        (double distance, double along, double length) = Measure(a, b, p);
        return distance <= _cell && along > 0.5 * _cell && along < length - (0.5 * _cell);
    }

    /// <summary>Within the distance tolerance of the segment, end points included, measured between the original points of the cells.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> lies on the segment.</returns>
    public bool IsOnSegment(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        if (p == a || p == b)
        {
            return true;
        }

        (double distance, double along, double length) = Measure(a, b, p);
        return distance <= _cell && along >= -_cell && along <= length + _cell;
    }

    /// <summary>Distance of the original point of <paramref name="p"/> from the line through the original points of <paramref name="a"/> and
    /// <paramref name="b"/>, its position along that segment from <paramref name="a"/>, and the segment's length, m.</summary>
    private (double Distance, double Along, double Length) Measure(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        Point2 start = Original(a);
        Point2 end = Original(b);
        Point2 point = Original(p);
        double dx = end.X - start.X;
        double dy = end.Y - start.Y;
        double length = Math.Sqrt((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
        double px = point.X - start.X;
        double py = point.Y - start.Y;
        return (Math.Abs((dx * py) - (dy * px)) / length, ((px * dx) + (py * dy)) / length, length);
    }
}
```

Only the T-junction test of `UnionTiles` (now `grid.IsStrictlyInside`) and its summary change.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Clipper2Lib;
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>
/// Polygon boolean operations on Clipper2 (ADR-002). Results are snapped to the <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> grid.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PolygonOps
{
    private readonly int _precision;
    private readonly double _distance;

    /// <summary>Creates the operations with the clipping precision of <paramref name="tolerances"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.DecimalPrecision"/> sets the snapping grid.</param>
    public PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _precision = tolerances.DecimalPrecision;
        _distance = tolerances.Distance;
    }

    /// <summary>Intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Intersect(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Execute(ClipType.Intersection, new[] { a }, new[] { b });

    /// <summary>Union of polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="polygons">Polygons to merge.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Union(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons) => Execute(ClipType.Union, polygons, Enumerable.Empty<Polygon2>());

    /// <summary>
    /// Union of polygons that tile a region and share their edges up to the distance tolerance, such as the zones of a storey (ADR-013). Before
    /// clipping, vertices in the same cell of the distance grid are unified, and every vertex lying on another tile's edge within the distance
    /// tolerance (a T-junction) is inserted into that edge, as <see cref="Layout.LayoutSurfaceBuilder"/> splits walls. Shared edges then cancel exactly;
    /// a plain <see cref="Union"/> of tiles that are not axis-aligned can leave slits or splits where rounding on the clipping grid moves a
    /// T-junction off its edge. Inserted vertices lie on their edges within the distance tolerance, so the outline is not moved.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="tiles">Polygons tiling a region.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> UnionTiles(IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> tiles)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in tiles.SelectMany(t => t.Rings).SelectMany(r => r))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        IEnumerable<Point2> Split(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ring.Count; i++)
            {
                GridPoint a = grid.Key(ring[i]);
                GridPoint b = grid.Key(ring[(i + 1) % ring.Count]);
                yield return grid.Original(a);
                if (a == b)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (GridPoint cut in vertices.Where(v => v != a && v != b && grid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v)).OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v)))
                {
                    yield return grid.Original(cut);
                }
            }
        }

        // A tile whose rings collapse on the grid cannot be split; it is clipped as given.
        Polygon2[] prepared = tiles
            .Select(t => Polygon2.Create(Split(t.Outer).ToArray(), t.Holes.Select(h => Split(h).ToArray()).ToArray()) is { IsSuccess: true } split ? split.Value : t)
            .ToArray();
        return Union(prepared);
    }

    /// <summary>Part of <paramref name="subject"/> not covered by <paramref name="clips"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="subject">Polygon to cut.</param>
    /// <param name="clips">Polygons to remove.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Difference(Polygon2 subject, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips) => Execute(ClipType.Difference, new[] { subject }, clips);

    /// <summary>Area of the intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Overlap area, m².</returns>
    public double IntersectionArea(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Intersect(a, b).Sum(p => p.Area);

    private IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Execute(ClipType clipType, IEnumerable<Polygon2> subjects, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips)
    {
        var clipper = new ClipperD(_precision);
        clipper.AddSubject(ToPaths(subjects));
        clipper.AddClip(ToPaths(clips));
        var tree = new PolyTreeD();
        clipper.Execute(clipType, FillRule.NonZero, tree);
        var result = new List<Polygon2>();
        Collect(tree, result);
        return result;
    }

    private static PathsD ToPaths(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons)
    {
        var paths = new PathsD();
        foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in polygons.SelectMany(p => p.Rings))
        {
            var path = new PathD(ring.Count);
            path.AddRange(ring.Select(p => new PointD(p.X, p.Y)));
            paths.Add(path);
        }

        return paths;
    }

    private static void Collect(PolyPathD parent, List<Polygon2> result)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < parent.Count; i++)
        {
            PolyPathD outer = parent[i];
            var holes = new List<IEnumerable<Point2>>();
            for (int j = 0; j < outer.Count; j++)
            {
                PolyPathD hole = outer[j];
                holes.Add(ToPoints(hole.Polygon!));
                Collect(hole, result);
            }

            Result<Polygon2> polygon = Polygon2.Create(ToPoints(outer.Polygon!), holes);
            if (polygon.IsSuccess)
            {
                result.Add(polygon.Value);
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<Point2> ToPoints(PathD path) => path.Select(p => new Point2(p.x, p.y));
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Layout/LayoutSurfaceBuilder.cs` (full file)**

The wall split calls `grid.IsStrictlyInside`, and `OnRing` takes the grid and calls `grid.IsOnSegment`.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Layout;

/// <summary>A zone footprint to build surfaces for.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone floor polygon.</param>
public sealed record LayoutZone(ZoneId Id, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>
/// Builds the surfaces of a single-storey layout: walls split wherever another zone's vertex touches them, paired across zones
/// (interzone), or on the plan boundary (outdoors), then merged into maximal collinear runs; plus one unresolved floor and ceiling per zone.
/// Edges that are neither shared nor on the boundary indicate gaps or overlaps and are errors.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LayoutSurfaceBuilder
{
    private readonly ToleranceSettings _tolerances;

    /// <summary>Creates the builder.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> is the vertex matching grid.</param>
    public LayoutSurfaceBuilder(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _tolerances = tolerances;
    }

    /// <summary>Builds walls (without windows), floors, and ceilings.</summary>
    /// <param name="footprint">Plan boundary; walls on it are outdoors.</param>
    /// <param name="zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
    /// <param name="height">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Surfaces grouped by zone (walls, floor, ceiling), or errors for gaps and overlaps.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> Build(Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, double height)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_tolerances.Distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r).Concat(zones.SelectMany(z => z.Footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r))))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        var segments = new List<Segment>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            int ringIndex = 0;
            foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in zones[zoneIndex].Footprint.Rings)
            {
                AddRingSegments(grid, vertices, zoneIndex, ringIndex++, ring, segments);
            }
        }

        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        Classify(grid, footprint, zones, segments, diagnostics);
        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(diagnostics);
        }

        var surfaces = new List<Surface>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            LayoutZone zone = zones[zoneIndex];
            int wallNumber = 1;
            foreach (IGrouping<int, Segment> ring in segments.Where(s => s.Zone == zoneIndex).GroupBy(s => s.Ring))
            {
                foreach (List<Segment> run in MergeRuns(ring.ToList()))
                {
                    Segment first = run[0];
                    surfaces.Add(new WallSurface(
                        new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/W{wallNumber++}"),
                        zone.Id,
                        grid.Original(first.A),
                        grid.Original(run[run.Count - 1].B),
                        elevation: 0.0,
                        height,
                        first.Boundary,
                        first.Adjacent is int adjacent ? zones[adjacent].Id : null,
                        Array.Empty<Window>()));
                }
            }

            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/F"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Floor, zone.Footprint, 0.0, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/C"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, zone.Footprint, height, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(surfaces);
    }

    private static void AddRingSegments(VertexGrid grid, GridPoint[] vertices, int zone, int ring, IReadOnlyList<Point2> points, List<Segment> segments)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
        {
            GridPoint a = grid.Key(points[i]);
            GridPoint b = grid.Key(points[(i + 1) % points.Count]);
            if (a == b)
            {
                continue;
            }

            GridPoint[] cuts = vertices
                .Where(v => v != a && v != b && grid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v))
                .OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v))
                .ToArray();
            GridPoint previous = a;
            foreach (GridPoint cut in cuts.Concat(new[] { b }))
            {
                segments.Add(new Segment(zone, ring, previous, cut));
                previous = cut;
            }
        }
    }

    private static void Classify(VertexGrid grid, Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, List<Segment> segments, List<Diagnostic> diagnostics)
    {
        var byDirection = segments.GroupBy(s => (s.A, s.B)).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.ToList());
        GridPoint[][] boundaryRings = footprint.Rings.Select(r => r.Select(grid.Key).ToArray()).ToArray();
        foreach (Segment segment in segments)
        {
            string subject = zones[segment.Zone].Id.Value;
            if (byDirection[(segment.A, segment.B)].Count > 1)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.OverlappingZones, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is used by more than one zone in the same direction.", subject));
                continue;
            }

            if (byDirection.TryGetValue((segment.B, segment.A), out List<Segment>? opposite))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Interzone;
                segment.Adjacent = opposite[0].Zone;
            }
            else if (boundaryRings.Any(ring => OnRing(grid, ring, segment.A, segment.B)))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Outdoors;
            }
            else
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is neither shared with another zone nor on the plan boundary (gap or overlap).", subject));
            }
        }
    }

    private IEnumerable<List<Segment>> MergeRuns(List<Segment> ring)
    {
        int start = Enumerable.Range(0, ring.Count).FirstOrDefault(i => !CanMerge(ring[(i + ring.Count - 1) % ring.Count], ring[i]));
        List<Segment>? run = null;
        for (int k = 0; k < ring.Count; k++)
        {
            Segment segment = ring[(start + k) % ring.Count];
            if (run is not null && CanMerge(run[run.Count - 1], segment))
            {
                run.Add(segment);
                continue;
            }

            if (run is not null)
            {
                yield return run;
            }

            run = new List<Segment> { segment };
        }

        if (run is not null)
        {
            yield return run;
        }
    }

    private bool CanMerge(Segment previous, Segment next)
    {
        if (previous.B != next.A || previous.Boundary != next.Boundary || previous.Adjacent != next.Adjacent)
        {
            return false;
        }

        double ax = previous.B.X - previous.A.X;
        double ay = previous.B.Y - previous.A.Y;
        double bx = next.B.X - next.A.X;
        double by = next.B.Y - next.A.Y;
        double lengths = Math.Sqrt((ax * ax) + (ay * ay)) * Math.Sqrt((bx * bx) + (by * by));
        double sine = ((ax * by) - (ay * bx)) / lengths;
        double cosine = ((ax * bx) + (ay * by)) / lengths;
        return cosine > 0 && Math.Abs(sine) <= Math.Sin(_tolerances.Angle);
    }

    private static bool OnRing(VertexGrid grid, GridPoint[] ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < ring.Length; i++)
        {
            GridPoint p = ring[i];
            GridPoint q = ring[(i + 1) % ring.Length];
            if (grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, a) && grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, b))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static string Describe(VertexGrid grid, Segment segment)
    {
        Point2 a = grid.Original(segment.A);
        Point2 b = grid.Original(segment.B);
        return $"({a.X:0.###}, {a.Y:0.###})-({b.X:0.###}, {b.Y:0.###})";
    }

    private sealed class Segment
    {
        public Segment(int zone, int ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
        {
            Zone = zone;
            Ring = ring;
            A = a;
            B = b;
        }

        public int Zone { get; }

        public int Ring { get; }

        public GridPoint A { get; }

        public GridPoint B { get; }

        public BoundaryCondition Boundary { get; set; } = BoundaryCondition.Unresolved;

        public int? Adjacent { get; set; }
    }
}
```

```markdown
**Edge matching.** Building the surfaces of a zoned plan snaps every vertex to the `Distance` grid, splits zone edges at other zones' vertices, pairs reversed edges of two zones as interzone walls, and marks edges on the plan boundary as outdoors. Any other edge indicates a gap or an overlap and is an error; geometry is never repaired silently (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6). Consecutive collinear segments with the same boundary merge into one wall.
```

```markdown
**Edge matching.** Building the surfaces of a zoned plan snaps every vertex to the `Distance` grid, splits zone edges at other zones' vertices, pairs reversed edges of two zones as interzone walls, and marks edges on the plan boundary as outdoors.
```
