**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PolygonOps.UnionTiles` and `VertexGrid` (Task 3); `GalleryBarGenerator`, `GalleryBarParameters.Default`, `GalleryBarPresets` (S8.1); `SemanticMerge` (Task 2); test support `Pipeline.Aggregator(string name)`, `Pipeline.Tolerances` (S4.2); `FloorEntry(IFloor, int)`, `BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings).Validate`; `DiagnosticCodes.FacadeNotCovered` (S3, D-041).
- Produces: `VertexGrid.Restore(Point2 p) : Point2` (the first point registered in the cell of `p`, or `p` when the cell is empty); `UnionTiles` maps every vertex of the clipped union through `Restore`, so the result lies on the tiles' own vertices. United outlines lie on their sources' façade lines, and uniting zones that were themselves united (the single-zone aggregators on a *Semantic Merge* floor) adds no rounding.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — replace `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Geometry/GeometryTests.cs` (full file)**

One new test, `UnionOfTilesKeepsTheTilesOwnVertices`: the four rotated and moved pinwheel tiles unite into one ring with one hole whose every vertex is one of the tiles' corners. Every other test is unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Geometry;

public sealed class GeometryTests
{
    private readonly PolygonOps _ops = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonNormalisesOrientationAndComputesAreaWithHoles()
    {
        Polygon2 polygon = Polygon2.Create(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(0, 10), new Point2(10, 10), new Point2(10, 0) },
            new[] { new[] { new Point2(2, 2), new Point2(4, 2), new Point2(4, 4), new Point2(2, 4) } }).Value;

        Assert.True(Polygon2.SignedArea(polygon.Outer) > 0);
        Assert.True(Polygon2.SignedArea(polygon.Holes[0]) < 0);
        Assert.Equal(96.0, polygon.Area, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonDropsClosingAndRepeatedPoints()
    {
        Polygon2 polygon = Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(1, 1), new Point2(0, 0) }).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3, polygon.Outer.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonRejectsDegenerateRings()
    {
        Assert.Contains(
            Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(2, 0) }).Diagnostics,
            d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.DegeneratePolygon);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void IntersectionOfOverlappingRectangles()
    {
        double area = _ops.IntersectionArea(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), Polygon2.Rectangle(5, 5, 15, 15));

        Assert.Equal(25.0, area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TouchingRectanglesDoNotIntersect()
    {
        Assert.Empty(_ops.Intersect(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 0, 20, 10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnionOfAdjacentRectanglesIsOnePolygon()
    {
        Polygon2 union = Assert.Single(_ops.Union(new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 0, 20, 5) }));

        Assert.Equal(100.0, union.Area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DifferenceKeepsHoles()
    {
        Polygon2 ring = Assert.Single(_ops.Difference(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(3, 3, 7, 7) }));

        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(84.0, ring.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0, 0, 10, 0, 180.0)]
    [InlineData(10, 0, 10, 10, 90.0)]
    [InlineData(10, 10, 0, 10, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0, 10, 0, 0, 270.0)]
    public void OutwardAzimuthOfCounterClockwiseRectangleEdges(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, double azimuth)
    {
        Assert.Equal(azimuth, Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(new Point2(x1, y1), new Point2(x2, y2)), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(44.9, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(45.0, OrientationBin.East)]
    [InlineData(180.0, OrientationBin.South)]
    [InlineData(270.0, OrientationBin.West)]
    [InlineData(315.0, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(-90.0, OrientationBin.West)]
    public void OrientationBins(double azimuth, OrientationBin bin)
    {
        Assert.Equal(bin, Orientation.Bin(azimuth));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CenteredWindowScalesTheWallRectangle()
    {
        Window window = Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 0.25 * 30.0)!;

        Assert.Equal(5.0, window.Width, 12);
        Assert.Equal(1.5, window.Height, 12);
        Assert.Equal(0.75, window.SillHeight, 12);
        Assert.Equal(7.5, window.Area, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ZeroGlazingHasNoWindow()
    {
        Assert.Null(Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 0.0));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingMustBeSmallerThanTheWall()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 30.0));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TranslateMovesEveryRing()
    {
        Polygon2 moved = Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 1, 1).Translate(2, 3);

        Assert.Equal(2.0, moved.Outer.Min(p => p.X));
        Assert.Equal(3.0, moved.Outer.Min(p => p.Y));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnionOfRotatedTilesWithTJunctionsIsOneOutline()
    {
        // A 4 m x 18 m strip and two 20 m x 9 m rectangles beside it: the rectangles' corners at (4, 9) lie on the strip's edge (a T-junction).
        // Rotated by 7.3°, rounding on the clipping grid moves that corner off the edge, which a plain union turns into a slit or a split.
        var motion = new PlanMotion(7.3, 0.0, 0.0);
        Polygon2[] tiles =
        {
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 4, 18).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(4, 0, 24, 9).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(4, 9, 24, 18).Transform(motion),
        };

        Polygon2 outline = Assert.Single(_ops.UnionTiles(tiles));

        Assert.Empty(outline.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(432.0, outline.Area, 5);
        var back = new PlanMotion(-7.3, 0.0, 0.0);
        Assert.All(outline.Outer.Select(back.Apply), p => Assert.True(
            Math.Abs(p.X) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.X - 24) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.Y) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.Y - 18) < 1e-5,
            $"({p.X}, {p.Y}) is not on the outline"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnionOfTilesKeepsTheTilesOwnVertices()
    {
        // Rotated and moved pinwheel tiles around a core: clipping rounds every vertex to the 1e-6 m grid, up to 0.7 grid units off the true
        // corner; the union of tiles returns the tiles' own vertices, so repeated unions do not accumulate rounding.
        var motion = new PlanMotion(131.4, 15.08, -153.9);
        Polygon2[] tiles =
        {
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 14, 8).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(14, 0, 22, 14).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(8, 14, 22, 22).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 8, 8, 22).Transform(motion),
        };
        Point2[] corners = tiles.SelectMany(t => t.Outer).ToArray();

        Polygon2 ring = Assert.Single(_ops.UnionTiles(tiles));

        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.All(ring.Rings.SelectMany(r => r), p => Assert.Contains(p, corners));
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/AggregatorTests.cs` (full file)**

One new theory, `SingleZoneMethodsCoverTheFacadesOfARotatedSemanticMergeFloor` (2 cases): the default gallery bar of S8.1, rotated by 321.2° and moved by (136.4, −118.8) m, through *Semantic Merge* and, given three times (1, 3, 1), to *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building*; each building is produced and passes validation. The usings `Lod.Core.Plans`, `Lod.Core.Programs`, and `Lod.Generators.GalleryBar` are added; every other test is unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class AggregatorTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void ZoneMultiplierCarriesMultipliersAndOrderedBoundaries()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 8), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { 1, 8, 1 }, building.Zones.GroupBy(z => z.Id.Value.Substring(0, 2)).Select(g => g.First().Multiplier));
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.All(horizontal.Where(h => h.Zone.Value.StartsWith("L0/") && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Floor), h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Ground, h.Boundary));
        Assert.All(horizontal.Where(h => h.Zone.Value.StartsWith("L9/") && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling), h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, h.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(
            horizontal.Length - 12,
            horizontal.Count(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic));
        Assert.Equal(10 * floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea), building.Zones.Sum(z => z.Multiplier * z.FloorArea), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RepresentativeStoreysKeepTheirTrueElevations()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 8), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        // Storeys 1-8 are represented by storey 5, the whole storey nearest their middle (D-045); the top storey stays storey 9.
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "L0/", "L5/", "L9/" }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value.Substring(0, 3)).Distinct());
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 0.0, 15.0, 27.0 }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Elevation).Distinct());
        Assert.Equal(30.0, Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces, building.OrientationDegrees).Height, 9);
        Assert.Equal("L0x1,L5x8,L9x1", building.Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storeys").Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ZoneMultiplierListsTheStoreysItRepresentsButDoesNotModel()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor, 1), new(floor, 8), new(floor, 1) };

        IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> unmodelled = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).UnmodelledStoreys(entries).Value;

        // Storey 5 represents storeys 1-8 (D-045); the other seven are represented but not modelled, at their true elevations.
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8 }, unmodelled.Select(s => s.Index));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 3.0, 6.0, 9.0, 12.0, 18.0, 21.0, 24.0 }, unmodelled.Select(s => s.Elevation));
        Assert.All(unmodelled, s => Assert.Equal(floor.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea), s.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea)));
        Assert.All(unmodelled.SelectMany(s => s.Zones), z => Assert.Equal(1, z.Multiplier));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ZoneMultiplierWithoutMultipliedStoreysModelsEveryStorey()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();

        Assert.Empty(new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).UnmodelledStoreys(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 2), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value);
        Assert.Contains(new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).UnmodelledStoreys(new FloorEntry[0]).Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.NoFloors);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(new[] { 5 }, "L0x1,L2x3,L4x1")]
    [InlineData(new[] { 2, 1 }, "L0x1,L1x1,L2x1")]
    [InlineData(new[] { 1, 8, 2 }, "L0x1,L5x8,L9x1,L10x1")]
    public void ExposedStoreysOfMultipliedTypesAreSplitOff(int[] multipliers, string storeys)
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(multipliers.Select(n => new FloorEntry(floor, n)).ToArray());
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(result.Value);

        Assert.Equal(storeys, result.Value.Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storeys").Value);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.FloorTypeSplit && d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Info);
        Assert.All(report.Checks, c => Assert.True(c.Passed, c.Check));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("setback", "wide x1, wide x4, narrow x3", "L0x1,L2x3,L4x1,L6x2,L7x1", "")]
    [InlineData("overhang", "narrow x2, wide x3", "L0x1,L1x1,L2x1,L3x1,L4x1", "L2")]
    public void VaryingFootprintsKeepExposedAreasWithoutMultiplyingThem(string name, string types, string storeys, string unsupported)
    {
        // Wide: 24 m x 18 m, two units per row. Narrow: 14 m x 18 m from the same west end, one unit per row. Either way 180 m² is exposed.
        IFloor wide = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IFloor narrow = Pipeline.DetailedFloor(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 });
        FloorEntry[] entries = types.Split(',')
            .Select(t => t.Trim().Split(new[] { " x" }, StringSplitOptions.None))
            .Select(t => new FloorEntry(t[0] == "wide" ? wide : narrow, int.Parse(t[1], System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)))
            .ToArray();

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries);
        IGeneratedBuilding building = result.Value;
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building);

        Assert.Equal(storeys, building.Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storeys").Value);
        Assert.Equal(unsupported, string.Join(",", result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey).Select(d => d.Subject)));
        Assert.All(report.Checks, c => Assert.True(c.Passed, $"{name}: {c.Check}"));
        Dictionary<ZoneId, int> multiplier = building.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id, z => z.Multiplier);
        HorizontalSurface[] exposed = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic).ToArray();
        Assert.All(exposed, h => Assert.Equal(1, multiplier[h.Zone]));
        double roof = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces, building.OrientationDegrees).Height;
        Assert.Equal(180.0, exposed.Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && h.Elevation < roof - 1.0).Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MultiplierTakesTheDesignationsOfItsStoreysFromTheStack()
    {
        // D-068: every floor and ceiling piece of a modelled storey is the stacked piece with the same ID; pieces between storeys become adiabatic.
        IFloor wide = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IFloor narrow = Pipeline.DetailedFloor(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 });
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(wide, 1), new(wide, 4), new(narrow, 3), new(wide, 2) };

        Dictionary<SurfaceId, HorizontalSurface> stacked = new Stack(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries).Value
            .Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToDictionary(h => h.Id);
        HorizontalSurface[] modelled = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries).Value
            .Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();

        Assert.NotEmpty(modelled);
        Assert.All(modelled, h =>
        {
            HorizontalSurface source = stacked[h.Id];
            Assert.Equal(source.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone ? BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic : source.Boundary, h.Boundary);
            Assert.Null(h.AdjacentZone);
            Assert.Equal(source.Zone, h.Zone);
            Assert.Equal(source.Kind, h.Kind);
            Assert.Equal(source.Elevation, h.Elevation);
            Assert.Equal(source.Area, h.Area, 9);
        });
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack")]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding")]
    public void AnOverhangingStoreyIsReportedAsUnsupported(string aggregator)
    {
        // Narrow (14 m) x2 below wide (24 m) x1: storey 2 overhangs storey 1 by 10 m x 18 m (D-074). The building is still produced and validates.
        IFloor wide = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IFloor narrow = Pipeline.DetailedFloor(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 });

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(narrow, 2), new FloorEntry(wide, 1) });

        Diagnostic warning = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, warning.Severity);
        Assert.Equal("L2", warning.Subject);
        Assert.Contains("storey 2", warning.Message);
        Assert.Contains("180.000000 m²", warning.Message);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(result.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack")]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding")]
    public void SetbacksAndIdenticalStoreysAreSupported(string aggregator)
    {
        IFloor wide = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IFloor narrow = Pipeline.DetailedFloor(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 });

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(wide, 3), new FloorEntry(narrow, 2) });

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BottomMiddleTopMultiplierMatchesTheStackExactly()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 8), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.All(report.Checks, c => Assert.True(c.Passed, c.Check));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeHasOneZonePerFloorEntry()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZonePerFloorType(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 1), new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 3) }).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E0", "E1" }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Floor type 0", "Floor type 1" }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 1, 3 }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Parts.Count));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 6, 18 }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.SourceZones.Count));
        Assert.All(building.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(1, z.Multiplier));
        Assert.Equal(3 * 432.0 * 3.0, building.Zones[1].Volume, 6);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 0.0, 3.0, 6.0, 9.0 }, building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).Select(p => p.Elevation));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeKeepsTheSlabsBetweenEntriesAsInterzonePairs()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZonePerFloorType(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 1), new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 3) }).Value;

        // Within E1 the slabs at 6 m and 9 m become internal mass (D-031); the slab at 3 m lies between E0 and E1 (D-068, D-071).
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("E1", 6.0), ("E1", 9.0) }, building.InternalMasses.Select(m => (m.Zone.Value, m.Elevation)));
        Assert.All(building.InternalMasses, m => Assert.Equal(432.0, m.SlabArea, 6));
        HorizontalSurface[] interzone = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();
        Assert.All(interzone, h => Assert.Equal(3.0, h.Elevation));
        Assert.Equal(432.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E0" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E1").Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
        Assert.Equal(432.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E1" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Floor && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E0").Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
        Assert.Equal(interzone.Length, interzone.Count(h => h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling) * 2);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeConservesEveryInvariant()
    {
        IFloor wide = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IFloor narrow = Pipeline.DetailedFloor(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 });

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZonePerFloorType(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(wide, 2), new FloorEntry(narrow, 3) }).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building);

        Assert.All(report.Checks.Where(c => c.Enforced), c => Assert.True(c.Passed, c.Check));
        Assert.All(building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OneFloorEntryGivesTheSingleZoneBuildingUnderAnotherName()
    {
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 3) };

        string perType = TextReport.Describe(new SingleZonePerFloorType(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries).Value);
        string building = TextReport.Describe(new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries).Value);

        Assert.Equal(building.Replace("BUILDING", "E0").Replace("\"Building\"", "\"Floor type 0\""), perType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingHasOneZoneWithOnePartPerStorey()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 3) }).Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(3, zone.Parts.Count);
        Assert.Equal(3 * 432.0 * 3.0, zone.Volume, 6);
        Assert.Equal(18, zone.SourceZones.Count);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Surfaces, s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingTurnsSlabsIntoInternalMass()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 3) }).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { 3.0, 6.0 }, building.InternalMasses.Select(m => m.Elevation));
        Assert.All(building.InternalMasses, m => Assert.Equal(432.0, m.SlabArea, 6));
        Assert.All(building.InternalMasses, m => Assert.Equal(2, m.ExposedFaces));
        Assert.All(building.InternalMasses, m => Assert.Equal(12, m.SourceSurfaces.Count));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingKeepsGroundRoofAndGlazing()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 3) }).Value;

        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.All(report.Checks.Where(c => c.Enforced), c => Assert.True(c.Passed, c.Check));
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Building.GroundArea" && c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Building.RoofArea" && c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Building.Height" && c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.Equal(3 * floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 6);
        Assert.Equal(12, building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count());
        Assert.All(building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
    }

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in new[] { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" }
        from aggregator in new[] { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" }
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EverySimplifierAndAggregatorCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor, 1), new(floor, 3), new(floor, 1) };

        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding")]
    public void SingleZoneMethodsCoverTheFacadesOfARotatedSemanticMergeFloor(string aggregator)
    {
        // Rotated by 321.2° and moved, a merged zone's corners on the clipping grid lie up to a grid unit off the true façade line; the storey
        // outline, united from those zones again, must still lie on the façade lines of the merged zones' walls (D-041).
        IGeneratedPlan bar = new GalleryBarGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default, new GalleryBarPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(PlanTransform.Apply(bar, 321.2, 136.4, -118.8).Value).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) });

        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MultiplierSnapshot()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(Pipeline.Simplifier("PerimeterCore"));
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 2), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(building), "aggregator-StackedFloorZoneMultiplier");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingSnapshot()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(Pipeline.DetailedFloor(), 2) }).Value;

        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(building), "aggregator-SingleZoneBuilding");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeSnapshot()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.DetailedFloor();
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZonePerFloorType(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 2) }).Value;

        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(building), "aggregator-SingleZonePerFloorType");
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Geometry/VertexGrid.cs` (full file)**

`Restore` is added; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>A cell of the <see cref="VertexGrid"/>, in grid units.</summary>
/// <param name="X">X index.</param>
/// <param name="Y">Y index.</param>
internal readonly record struct GridPoint(long X, long Y);

/// <summary>
/// Snaps points to the distance grid, remembers the first original point of each grid cell, and tests whether a grid point lies on a segment
/// within the distance tolerance. Shared by the layout surface builder and the union of tiles, so both recognise the same vertices and T-junctions.
/// The segment tests measure the cells' original points, not the snapped cells: snapping a point and both ends of a segment it lies on moves the
/// point up to √2 grid units off the snapped segment, so on rotated plans a T-junction exactly on an edge could fail a test on snapped cells.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class VertexGrid
{
    private readonly double _cell;
    private readonly Dictionary<GridPoint, Point2> _originals = new();

    /// <summary>Creates an empty grid.</summary>
    /// <param name="cell">Grid spacing, m: the distance tolerance.</param>
    public VertexGrid(double cell)
    {
        _cell = cell;
    }

    /// <summary>Registered cells, in registration order.</summary>
    public IEnumerable<GridPoint> Keys => _originals.Keys;

    /// <summary>The cell of a point.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The cell.</returns>
    public GridPoint Key(Point2 p) => new((long)Math.Round(p.X / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero), (long)Math.Round(p.Y / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));

    /// <summary>Registers a point; the first point registered in a cell stands for the cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    public void Register(Point2 p)
    {
        GridPoint key = Key(p);
        if (!_originals.ContainsKey(key))
        {
            _originals.Add(key, p);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The first point registered in a cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="key">The cell.</param>
    /// <returns>The point.</returns>
    public Point2 Original(GridPoint key) => _originals[key];

    /// <summary>The first point registered in the cell of <paramref name="p"/>, or <paramref name="p"/> itself when its cell is empty.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The registered point.</returns>
    public Point2 Restore(Point2 p) => _originals.TryGetValue(Key(p), out Point2 original) ? original : p;

    /// <summary>Position of <paramref name="p"/> along <paramref name="a"/>–<paramref name="b"/>, 0 at <paramref name="a"/> and 1 at <paramref name="b"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The parameter.</returns>
    public static double Parameter(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X;
        double dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        return (((p.X - a.X) * dx) + ((p.Y - a.Y) * dy)) / ((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Within the distance tolerance of the line and strictly between the end points (by more than half the tolerance), measured between the
    /// original points of the cells.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> splits the segment.</returns>
    public bool IsStrictlyInside(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        (double distance, double along, double length) = Measure(a, b, p);
        return distance <= _cell && along > 0.5 * _cell && along < length - (0.5 * _cell);
    }

    /// <summary>Within the distance tolerance of the segment, end points included, measured between the original points of the cells.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> lies on the segment.</returns>
    public bool IsOnSegment(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        if (p == a || p == b)
        {
            return true;
        }

        (double distance, double along, double length) = Measure(a, b, p);
        return distance <= _cell && along >= -_cell && along <= length + _cell;
    }

    /// <summary>Distance of the original point of <paramref name="p"/> from the line through the original points of <paramref name="a"/> and
    /// <paramref name="b"/>, its position along that segment from <paramref name="a"/>, and the segment's length, m.</summary>
    private (double Distance, double Along, double Length) Measure(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        Point2 start = Original(a);
        Point2 end = Original(b);
        Point2 point = Original(p);
        double dx = end.X - start.X;
        double dy = end.Y - start.Y;
        double length = Math.Sqrt((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
        double px = point.X - start.X;
        double py = point.Y - start.Y;
        return (Math.Abs((dx * py) - (dy * px)) / length, ((px * dx) + (py * dy)) / length, length);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Geometry/PolygonOps.cs` (full file)**

`UnionTiles` maps the union's vertices through `grid.Restore`; when a restored ring is not a valid polygon, the clipped one is kept.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Clipper2Lib;
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>
/// Polygon boolean operations on Clipper2 (ADR-002). Results are snapped to the <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> grid.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PolygonOps
{
    private readonly int _precision;
    private readonly double _distance;

    /// <summary>Creates the operations with the clipping precision of <paramref name="tolerances"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.DecimalPrecision"/> sets the snapping grid.</param>
    public PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _precision = tolerances.DecimalPrecision;
        _distance = tolerances.Distance;
    }

    /// <summary>Intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Intersect(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Execute(ClipType.Intersection, new[] { a }, new[] { b });

    /// <summary>Union of polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="polygons">Polygons to merge.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Union(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons) => Execute(ClipType.Union, polygons, Enumerable.Empty<Polygon2>());

    /// <summary>
    /// Union of polygons that tile a region and share their edges up to the distance tolerance, such as the zones of a storey (ADR-013). Before
    /// clipping, vertices in the same cell of the distance grid are unified, and every vertex lying on another tile's edge within the distance
    /// tolerance (a T-junction) is inserted into that edge, as <see cref="Layout.LayoutSurfaceBuilder"/> splits walls. Shared edges then cancel exactly;
    /// a plain <see cref="Union"/> of tiles that are not axis-aligned can leave slits or splits where rounding on the clipping grid moves a
    /// T-junction off its edge. Inserted vertices lie on their edges within the distance tolerance, so the outline is not moved. The result keeps the
    /// tiles' own vertices: clipping rounds every vertex to the grid, up to 0.7 grid units off, so each result vertex is replaced by the tile
    /// vertex of its grid cell. United outlines therefore lie on the tiles' edges, and uniting zones that were themselves united adds no rounding.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="tiles">Polygons tiling a region.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> UnionTiles(IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> tiles)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in tiles.SelectMany(t => t.Rings).SelectMany(r => r))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        IEnumerable<Point2> Split(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ring.Count; i++)
            {
                GridPoint a = grid.Key(ring[i]);
                GridPoint b = grid.Key(ring[(i + 1) % ring.Count]);
                yield return grid.Original(a);
                if (a == b)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (GridPoint cut in vertices.Where(v => v != a && v != b && grid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v)).OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v)))
                {
                    yield return grid.Original(cut);
                }
            }
        }

        // A tile whose rings collapse on the grid cannot be split; it is clipped as given.
        Polygon2[] prepared = tiles
            .Select(t => Polygon2.Create(Split(t.Outer).ToArray(), t.Holes.Select(h => Split(h).ToArray()).ToArray()) is { IsSuccess: true } split ? split.Value : t)
            .ToArray();
        return Union(prepared)
            .Select(u => Polygon2.Create(u.Outer.Select(grid.Restore), u.Holes.Select(h => h.Select(grid.Restore))) is { IsSuccess: true } restored ? restored.Value : u)
            .ToArray();
    }

    /// <summary>Part of <paramref name="subject"/> not covered by <paramref name="clips"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="subject">Polygon to cut.</param>
    /// <param name="clips">Polygons to remove.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Difference(Polygon2 subject, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips) => Execute(ClipType.Difference, new[] { subject }, clips);

    /// <summary>Area of the intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Overlap area, m².</returns>
    public double IntersectionArea(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Intersect(a, b).Sum(p => p.Area);

    private IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Execute(ClipType clipType, IEnumerable<Polygon2> subjects, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips)
    {
        var clipper = new ClipperD(_precision);
        clipper.AddSubject(ToPaths(subjects));
        clipper.AddClip(ToPaths(clips));
        var tree = new PolyTreeD();
        clipper.Execute(clipType, FillRule.NonZero, tree);
        var result = new List<Polygon2>();
        Collect(tree, result);
        return result;
    }

    private static PathsD ToPaths(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons)
    {
        var paths = new PathsD();
        foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in polygons.SelectMany(p => p.Rings))
        {
            var path = new PathD(ring.Count);
            path.AddRange(ring.Select(p => new PointD(p.X, p.Y)));
            paths.Add(path);
        }

        return paths;
    }

    private static void Collect(PolyPathD parent, List<Polygon2> result)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < parent.Count; i++)
        {
            PolyPathD outer = parent[i];
            var holes = new List<IEnumerable<Point2>>();
            for (int j = 0; j < outer.Count; j++)
            {
                PolyPathD hole = outer[j];
                holes.Add(ToPoints(hole.Polygon!));
                Collect(hole, result);
            }

            Result<Polygon2> polygon = Polygon2.Create(ToPoints(outer.Polygon!), holes);
            if (polygon.IsSuccess)
            {
                result.Add(polygon.Value);
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<Point2> ToPoints(PathD path) => path.Select(p => new Point2(p.x, p.y));
}
```

A plain union of footprints that meet at T-junctions, such as the pinwheel dwellings of the point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), splits the group or leaves slits once the plan is rotated off the axes, because rounding on the clipping grid moves the T-junction off its edge. A plain union of footprints that meet at T-junctions, such as the pinwheel dwellings of the point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), splits the group or leaves slits once the plan is rotated off the axes, because rounding on the clipping grid moves the T-junction off its edge. A united zone may have holes, where it surrounds zones of another type (the point plate's dwellings around the core). The union keeps the tiles' own vertices: clipping rounds every vertex to the grid, up to 0.7 grid units off its true position, so each vertex of the result is replaced by the tile vertex of its grid cell. United zones therefore lie on their sources' façade lines, and uniting zones that were themselves united, as the single-zone aggregators do with a *Semantic Merge* floor, adds no rounding; without this, a rotated *Semantic Merge* floor could fail *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* with `FacadeNotCovered` (S8.2).

### Task 5: Winged band generator (`HDHM-TYP-003`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/WingedBand/WingedBandGenerator.cs`
- Modify: `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (S8.1 file)
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/WingedBand/WingedBandGeneratorTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/WingedBand/Snapshots/winged-band-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StairBayLayout.Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth) : PlanLayout` (S8.1, internal), the `PlanGenerator` base and records as in Task 1, `Polygon2.Create(IEnumerable<Point2>) : Result<Polygon2>`, `GeometryLimits` and the info `WindowOmitted` (S8.1, ADR-011), `PlanAssertions` (S8.1).
- Produces:
  - In `StairBayLayout` (internal): `Wing(string prefix, string namePrefix, double x0, double y0, double length, double depth, bool alongY, int bays, double unitWidth, double stairWidth) : IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone>` (bays from (x0, y0) along +x, or +y when `alongY`; zones `{prefix}ST1…`, then `{prefix}U1…`, numbered along the wing; the last bay ends exactly at the wing's length); `Fit(double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth) : (int Bays, double UnitWidth)` (`n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))`, rounded half away from zero, and the adjusted width `(L − n·S) / (2n)`); `Need(int bays, double stairWidth) : string` (the stairs a fitted wing needs, for messages; replaced in Task 6). `Bays` returns the footprint rectangle and `Wing(string.Empty, string.Empty, 0, 0, length, depth, alongY: false, …)`, the same zones as in S8.1.
  - `public sealed record WingedBandParameters(int WingCount, double WingLength, double WingSpacing, double Depth, double UnitWidth, double StairWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (3, 17, 17, 12, 7, 3, 3, 0); `public sealed record WingedBandPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets`; `public sealed class WingedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `WingedBandGenerator`: a band `[0, W·D + (W − 1)·g] × [0, D]` of stair bays along plan x and `WingCount` wings `[(i − 1)(D + g), (i − 1)(D + g) + D] × [D, D + WingLength]` of stair bays projecting to plan north, the first flush with the band's west end and the last with its east end; zones `B-ST1…`, `B-U1…` (*Band Stair 1*, *Band Unit 1*, …), then per wing `W{i}-ST1…`, `W{i}-U1…` (*Wing 2 Stair 1*, …); the footprint is the comb outline; errors `InvalidParameter` for `WingCount` < 2 (`WingCount must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got {n}.`), for each length that is not positive and finite, and, only when those pass, for the band or a wing whose stairs leave no dwelling width (`StairWidth 10 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m wing: its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.`); provenance keys `WingCount`, `WingLength`, `WingSpacing`, `Depth`, `UnitWidth`, `StairWidth`.

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/WingedBand/WingedBandGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

27 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (21 zone IDs, 1452 m², names, stairs 36 m², band dwellings 7.25 m × 12 m, wing dwellings 84 m², corners of `B-ST2`, `W2-U1`, `W2-ST1`, `W2-U2`, no source zones); the comb outline without holes; tiling for four wing counts, lengths, and spacings; every wing adjoining the band along its whole 12 m root; every stair serving the dwellings of its bay; the band's north façade as eight fragments of 5.5, 7.25, 3, 1.25, 1.25, 3, 7.25, and 5.5 m; the window rule; with `WingSpacing` 14 m the two 0.5 m fragments without a window and their `WindowOmitted` infos; seven invalid parameters; one wing; stairs that leave no dwelling width; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.WingedBand;

public sealed class WingedBandGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly WingedBandPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "B-ST1", "B-ST2", "B-ST3", "B-ST4", "B-U1", "B-U2", "B-U3", "B-U4", "B-U5", "B-U6", "B-U7", "B-U8",
        "W1-ST1", "W1-U1", "W1-U2", "W2-ST1", "W2-U1", "W2-U2", "W3-ST1", "W3-U1", "W3-U2",
    };

    private readonly WingedBandGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new WingedBandParameters(WingCount: 3, WingLength: 17.0, WingSpacing: 17.0, Depth: 12.0, UnitWidth: 7.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            WingedBandParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsABandOfFourStairBaysWithThreeOneBayWings()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((70.0 * 12.0) + (3 * 17.0 * 12.0), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Band Stair 2", "Band Unit 3", "Wing 2 Stair 1", "Wing 2 Unit 2" }, plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "B-ST2" or "B-U3" or "W2-ST1" or "W2-U2").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(36.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("B-U")), z => Assert.Equal(7.25 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("W") && z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal(84.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(24.75, 0.0), new Point2(27.75, 0.0), new Point2(27.75, 12.0), new Point2(24.75, 12.0) }, Outer(plan, "B-ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(29.0, 12.0), new Point2(41.0, 12.0), new Point2(41.0, 19.0), new Point2(29.0, 19.0) }, Outer(plan, "W2-U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(29.0, 19.0), new Point2(41.0, 19.0), new Point2(41.0, 22.0), new Point2(29.0, 22.0) }, Outer(plan, "W2-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(29.0, 22.0), new Point2(41.0, 22.0), new Point2(41.0, 29.0), new Point2(29.0, 29.0) }, Outer(plan, "W2-U2"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheFootprintIsACombOpenToPlanNorth()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(70, 0), new Point2(70, 29), new Point2(58, 29), new Point2(58, 12), new Point2(41, 12),
                new Point2(41, 29), new Point2(29, 29), new Point2(29, 12), new Point2(12, 12), new Point2(12, 29), new Point2(0, 29),
            },
            plan.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Empty(plan.Footprint.Holes);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, 17.0, 17.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 17.0, 17.0)]
    [InlineData(5, 30.0, 9.5)]
    [InlineData(4, 8.0, 40.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int wings, double wingLength, double wingSpacing)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default with { WingCount = wings, WingLength = wingLength, WingSpacing = wingSpacing });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(wings, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value.Split('-')[0]).Distinct().Count(p => p.StartsWith("W")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryWingAdjoinsTheBandAlongItsWholeRoot()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default);

        foreach (string wing in new[] { "W1", "W2", "W3" })
        {
            double root = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
                .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == $"{wing}-U1" && w.AdjacentZone is { } adjacent && adjacent.Value.StartsWith("B-"))
                .Sum(w => w.Length);
            Assert.Equal(12.0, root, 9);
        }

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-ST1", "B-U1", "B-U2", "W1-ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W1-U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-U4", "B-U5", "W2-ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W2-U1"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStairServesTheDwellingsOfItsBay()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-U3", "B-U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "B-ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-U1", "B-U2", "W1-U1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "B-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "W2-U1", "W2-U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W2-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "W2-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "W2-U2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheBandNorthFacadeIsFragmentsBetweenTheWings()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default);
        WallSurface[] north = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("B-") && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.North)
            .OrderBy(w => w.End.X)
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-U2", "B-U3", "B-ST2", "B-U4", "B-U5", "B-ST3", "B-U6", "B-U7" }, north.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 5.5, 7.25, 3.0, 1.25, 1.25, 3.0, 7.25, 5.5 }, north.Select(w => System.Math.Round(w.Length, 9)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FragmentsBelowTheMinimumWallLengthGetNoWindow()
    {
        // 14 m between the wings: the band's bay boundaries leave 0.5 m of north façade to the dwellings beside the middle wing's root.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default with { WingSpacing = 14.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        WallSurface[] fragments = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Length < 1.0).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B-U4", "B-U5" }, fragments.Select(w => w.Zone.Value).OrderBy(z => z, System.StringComparer.Ordinal));
        Assert.All(fragments, w => Assert.Equal(0.5, w.Length, 9));
        Assert.All(fragments, w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(fragments.Select(w => w.Id.Value), result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "WingCount")]
    [InlineData(0, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "WingCount")]
    [InlineData(3, 0.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "WingLength")]
    [InlineData(3, 17.0, -1.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "WingSpacing")]
    [InlineData(3, 17.0, 17.0, double.NaN, 7.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(3, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 0.0, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, double.PositiveInfinity, "StairWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(int wings, double wingLength, double wingSpacing, double depth, double unitWidth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new WingedBandParameters(wings, wingLength, wingSpacing, depth, unitWidth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OneWingHasNoRepeatedWingToBandRelation()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default with { WingCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("WingCount must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got 1.", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StairsThatLeaveNoRoomForDwellingsAreAnError()
    {
        // A 17 m wing with 10 m stairs: round(17 / 11) = 2 bays need 20 m of stairs, so no dwelling width is left; the band still fits.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default with { UnitWidth = 0.5, StairWidth = 10.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal("StairWidth 10 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m wing: its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new WingedBandParameters(1, -17.0, 0.0, 0.0, -7.0, 0.0, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(6, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default, new WingedBandPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(WingedBandGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7",
                "WingCount=3", "WingLength=17", "WingSpacing=17",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default)), "winged-band-canonical");
    }

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(WingedBandParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (full file)**

`Bays` keeps its signature and its zones: the stair bay bar and the stair pair are unchanged, and their snapshots stay byte-identical.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>
/// The stair bay of ADR-006: dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each through the full depth, the stair serving the two dwellings
/// beside it. Shared by <see cref="StairBayBarGenerator"/> (HDHM-TYP-001), the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008), and the wings of the winged band
/// (HDHM-TYP-003) and the open court (HDHM-TYP-004), so the bay geometry exists once.
/// </summary>
internal static class StairBayLayout
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays west to east from the origin. Zones: <c>ST1…STn</c> (stairs, west to east), then <c>U1…U2n</c>
    /// (dwellings, west to east); bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Bar depth, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The footprint and the zones.</returns>
    public static PlanLayout Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double length = bays * ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, depth), Wing(string.Empty, string.Empty, 0.0, 0.0, length, depth, alongY: false, bays, unitWidth, stairWidth));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The stair bays that fill a wing of length <paramref name="length"/> exactly (ADR-006): <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays, rounded
    /// half away from zero, with the dwelling width adjusted to <c>(L − n·S) / (2n)</c>, which is not positive when the stairs leave no room.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <returns>The bay count and the adjusted dwelling width.</returns>
    public static (int Bays, double UnitWidth) Fit(double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double bays = Math.Max(1.0, Math.Round(length / ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth), MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
        int count = bays >= int.MaxValue ? int.MaxValue : (int)bays;
        return (count, (length - (count * stairWidth)) / (2.0 * count));
    }

    /// <summary>The stair length a fitted wing needs, for messages: "its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs".</summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Bay count.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The phrase.</returns>
    public static string Need(int bays, double stairWidth) => bays == 1
        ? $"its one stair bay needs {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m of stairs"
        : $"its {bays} stair bays need {Provenance.Format(bays * stairWidth)} m of stairs";

    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays along a wing that starts at (<paramref name="x0"/>, <paramref name="y0"/>) and runs along +x, or
    /// along +y when <paramref name="alongY"/>; the last bay ends exactly at the wing's length. Zones: <c>{prefix}ST1…</c>, then <c>{prefix}U1…</c>,
    /// numbered along the wing's axis; bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="prefix">Zone ID prefix, such as <c>B-</c>.</param>
    /// <param name="namePrefix">Display name prefix, such as <c>Band </c>.</param>
    /// <param name="x0">Wing start, plan x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="y0">Wing start, plan y, m.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length along its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Wing depth across its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="alongY">Whether the wing runs along +y instead of +x.</param>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The stairs, then the dwellings.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Wing(
        string prefix,
        string namePrefix,
        double x0,
        double y0,
        double length,
        double depth,
        bool alongY,
        int bays,
        double unitWidth,
        double stairWidth)
    {
        double bayWidth = (2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth;
        double start = alongY ? y0 : x0;
        Polygon2 Piece(double from, double to) => alongY ? Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, from, x0 + depth, to) : Polygon2.Rectangle(from, y0, to, y0 + depth);
        var stairs = new List<PlannedZone>();
        var units = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < bays; i++)
        {
            double west = start + (i * bayWidth);
            double stairWest = west + unitWidth;
            double stairEast = stairWest + stairWidth;
            double east = i == bays - 1 ? start + length : west + bayWidth;
            stairs.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}ST{i + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Stair {i + 1}", SpaceType.Stair, Piece(stairWest, stairEast)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(west, stairWest)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 2}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 2}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(stairEast, east)));
        }

        stairs.AddRange(units);
        return stairs;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/WingedBand/WingedBandGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.WingedBand;

/// <summary>Parameters of the winged band (HDHM-TYP-003, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="WingCount">Number of wings projecting from the band; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="WingLength">Length of each wing from the band to its tip, m.</param>
/// <param name="WingSpacing">Clear distance between neighbouring wings along the band, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Depth of the band and of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the band and the wings, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill each exactly.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the band and the wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record WingedBandParameters(
    int WingCount,
    double WingLength,
    double WingSpacing,
    double Depth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 3 wings of 17 m, 17 m apart, 12 m deep, 7 m dwellings and 3 m stairs, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static WingedBandParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the winged band, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record WingedBandPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Projecting wings joined by a common band (HDHM-TYP-003, ADR-006): a band of stair bays along plan x and <see cref="WingedBandParameters.WingCount"/>
/// wings of stair bays projecting to plan north, the first flush with the band's west end and the last with its east end. Every wing adjoins the band
/// along its whole root (R001), and at least two wings repeat the wing-to-band relation (R002); one wing is rejected (R003, R004). The outline is a
/// comb, so the band's north façade is fragments between the wings' roots and its bay boundaries; ADR-011 governs their windows. Each wing and the band
/// hold <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays with the dwelling width adjusted to fill their length <c>L</c>. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// With <c>D</c> the depth and <c>g</c> the wing spacing, the band is <c>[0, W·D + (W − 1)·g] × [0, D]</c> and wing <c>i</c> (from 1) is
/// <c>[(i − 1)(D + g), (i − 1)(D + g) + D] × [D, D + WingLength]</c>. Zone IDs: <c>B-ST1…</c>, <c>B-U1…</c> (band, west to east), then per wing
/// <c>W{i}-ST1…</c>, <c>W{i}-U1…</c> (south to north); display names <c>Band Unit 3</c>, <c>Wing 2 Stair 1</c>.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class WingedBandGenerator : PlanGenerator<WingedBandParameters, WingedBandPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public WingedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(WingedBandGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(WingedBandParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (parameters.WingCount < 2)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingCount)} must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got {parameters.WingCount}."));
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingLength), parameters.WingLength),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingSpacing), parameters.WingSpacing),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        errors.AddRange(values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)).Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}.")));
        if (errors.Count == 0)
        {
            errors.AddRange(WingFits("the", "band", BandLength(parameters), parameters));
            errors.AddRange(WingFits("each", "wing", parameters.WingLength, parameters));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(WingedBandParameters p)
    {
        double bandLength = BandLength(p);
        double top = p.Depth + p.WingLength;
        (int bandBays, double bandUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(bandLength, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        (int wingBays, double wingUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(p.WingLength, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>(StairBayLayout.Wing("B-", "Band ", 0.0, 0.0, bandLength, p.Depth, alongY: false, bandBays, bandUnit, p.StairWidth));
        for (int i = 1; i <= p.WingCount; i++)
        {
            zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing($"W{i}-", $"Wing {i} ", WingWest(p, i), p.Depth, p.WingLength, p.Depth, alongY: true, wingBays, wingUnit, p.StairWidth));
        }

        var outline = new List<Point2> { new(0.0, 0.0), new(bandLength, 0.0) };
        for (int i = p.WingCount; i >= 1; i--)
        {
            double west = WingWest(p, i);
            outline.Add(new Point2(west + p.Depth, top));
            outline.Add(new Point2(west, top));
            if (i > 1)
            {
                outline.Add(new Point2(west, p.Depth));
                outline.Add(new Point2(WingWest(p, i - 1) + p.Depth, p.Depth));
            }
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Create(outline).Value, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(WingedBandParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingCount), p.WingCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingLength), Provenance.Format(p.WingLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingSpacing), Provenance.Format(p.WingSpacing)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    private static double BandLength(WingedBandParameters p) => WingWest(p, p.WingCount) + p.Depth;

    private static double WingWest(WingedBandParameters p, int wing) => (wing - 1) * (p.Depth + p.WingSpacing);

    private static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> WingFits(string article, string wing, double length, WingedBandParameters p)
    {
        (int bays, double unitWidth) = StairBayLayout.Fit(length, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        if (!IsPositive(unitWidth))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(WingedBandParameters.StairWidth)} {Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)} m leaves no dwelling width in {article} {Provenance.Format(length)} m {wing}: "
                + $"{StairBayLayout.Need(bays, p.StairWidth)}; reduce {nameof(WingedBandParameters.StairWidth)} or {nameof(WingedBandParameters.UnitWidth)}.");
        }
    }
}
```
