### Task 6: Open court generator (`HDHM-TYP-004`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/OpenCourt/OpenCourtGenerator.cs`
- Modify: `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs`, `src/Lod.Generators/WingedBand/WingedBandGenerator.cs` (Task 5)
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OpenCourt/OpenCourtGeneratorTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OpenCourt/Snapshots/open-court-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StairBayLayout.Wing`, `Fit` (Task 5); the `PlanGenerator` base and records as in Task 1; `PolygonOps.IntersectionArea` (in the test that the court is empty); `PlanAssertions` (S8.1).
- Produces:
  - In `StairBayLayout`: `FitErrors(string wing, double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth) : IEnumerable<Diagnostic>` (zero or one `InvalidParameter` error; the message names the wing, such as `each 17 m wing`, and the stairs it would need) replaces `Need`; `WingedBandGenerator` calls it for the band and the wings, with messages unchanged, and loses its private `WingFits`.
  - `public sealed record OpenCourtParameters(double CourtWidth, double CourtDepth, double Depth, double UnitWidth, double StairWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (24, 17, 12, 7, 3, 3, 0); `public sealed record OpenCourtPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets`; `public sealed class OpenCourtGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `OpenCourtGenerator`: west wing `[0, D] × [0, c]`, east wing `[D + w, 2D + w] × [0, c]`, north wing `[0, 2D + w] × [c, c + D]`, each a bar of stair bays, around a court `[D, D + w] × [0, c]` open to plan south; zones `N-ST1…`, `N-U1…` (*North Stair 1*, …, west to east), then `W-…` and `E-…` (*West Unit 1*, …, south to north); the footprint is the U outline; `InvalidParameter` for each length that is not positive and finite and, only when those pass, for the north wing or the side wings whose stairs leave no dwelling width (`StairWidth 20 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m side wing: its one stair bay needs 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.`); provenance keys `CourtWidth`, `CourtDepth`, `Depth`, `UnitWidth`, `StairWidth`.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OpenCourt/OpenCourtGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

22 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (15 zone IDs, 984 m², names, stairs 36 m², north dwellings 6.5 m × 12 m, side dwellings 84 m², corners of `N-ST2`, `W-ST1`, `E-U2`, no source zones); the U outline without holes and the empty court; tiling for three court sizes; dwellings on three sides of the court (24 m of the north wing's south façade, the inner façades of the side wings); every dwelling adjoining a stair; the window rule; six invalid parameters; stairs that leave no dwelling width; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.OpenCourt;

public sealed class OpenCourtGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly OpenCourtPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "N-ST1", "N-ST2", "N-ST3", "N-U1", "N-U2", "N-U3", "N-U4", "N-U5", "N-U6",
        "W-ST1", "W-U1", "W-U2", "E-ST1", "E-U1", "E-U2",
    };

    private readonly OpenCourtGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new OpenCourtParameters(CourtWidth: 24.0, CourtDepth: 17.0, Depth: 12.0, UnitWidth: 7.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            OpenCourtParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsThreeStairBayWingsAroundACourt()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((48.0 * 29.0) - (24.0 * 17.0), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "North Stair 2", "North Unit 3", "West Unit 2", "East Stair 1" }, plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "N-ST2" or "N-U3" or "W-U2" or "E-ST1").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(36.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("N-U")), z => Assert.Equal(6.5 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => !z.Id.Value.StartsWith("N-") && z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal(84.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(22.5, 17.0), new Point2(25.5, 17.0), new Point2(25.5, 29.0), new Point2(22.5, 29.0) }, Outer(plan, "N-ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 7.0), new Point2(12.0, 7.0), new Point2(12.0, 10.0), new Point2(0.0, 10.0) }, Outer(plan, "W-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(36.0, 10.0), new Point2(48.0, 10.0), new Point2(48.0, 17.0), new Point2(36.0, 17.0) }, Outer(plan, "E-U2"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheFootprintIsAUOpenToPlanSouth()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(12, 0), new Point2(12, 17), new Point2(36, 17), new Point2(36, 0), new Point2(48, 0), new Point2(48, 29), new Point2(0, 29) },
            plan.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Empty(plan.Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default).IntersectionArea(plan.Footprint, Polygon2.Rectangle(12, 0, 36, 17)), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(24.0, 17.0)]
    [InlineData(6.0, 40.0)]
    [InlineData(50.0, 4.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(double courtWidth, double courtDepth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default with { CourtWidth = courtWidth, CourtDepth = courtDepth });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "N", "W", "E" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value.Split('-')[0]).Distinct());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DwellingsLieOnThreeSidesOfTheCourt()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default);
        WallSurface[] court = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Start.X is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0 && w.End.X is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0 && w.Start.Y <= 17.0 && w.End.Y <= 17.0)
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "N-U2", "N-U3", "N-ST2", "N-U4", "N-U5" }, court.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.South).OrderBy(w => w.End.X).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "W-U1", "W-ST1", "W-U2" }, court.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.East).OrderBy(w => w.Start.Y).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E-U1", "E-ST1", "E-U2" }, court.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.West).OrderBy(w => w.End.Y).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(24.0, court.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.South).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryDwellingAdjoinsAStair()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.All(
            plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit),
            z => Assert.Contains(PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, z.Id.Value), n => plan.Zones.Single(s => s.Id.Value == n).SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "N-ST1", "N-U1", "N-U2", "W-ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W-U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E-ST1", "N-ST3", "N-U5", "N-U6" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "E-U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "W-U1", "W-U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "W-U1"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, -17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtDepth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 17.0, 0.0, 7.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 17.0, 12.0, double.NaN, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, -3.0, "StairWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double courtWidth, double courtDepth, double depth, double unitWidth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OpenCourtParameters(courtWidth, courtDepth, depth, unitWidth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StairsThatLeaveNoRoomForDwellingsAreAnError()
    {
        // 17 m side wings with 20 m stairs: one bay needs more than the wing's length; the 48 m north wing (round(48 / 21) = 2 bays) still fits.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default with { UnitWidth = 0.5, StairWidth = 20.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal("StairWidth 20 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m side wing: its one stair bay needs 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OpenCourtParameters(0.0, 0.0, -12.0, 0.0, 0.0, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(5, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default, new OpenCourtPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(OpenCourtGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "CourtDepth=17", "CourtWidth=24", "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default)), "open-court-canonical");
    }

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(OpenCourtParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (full file)**

`Need` becomes `FitErrors`, which builds the whole error; `Wing`, `Fit`, and `Bays` are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>
/// The stair bay of ADR-006: dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each through the full depth, the stair serving the two dwellings
/// beside it. Shared by <see cref="StairBayBarGenerator"/> (HDHM-TYP-001), the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008), and the wings of the winged band
/// (HDHM-TYP-003) and the open court (HDHM-TYP-004), so the bay geometry exists once.
/// </summary>
internal static class StairBayLayout
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays west to east from the origin. Zones: <c>ST1…STn</c> (stairs, west to east), then <c>U1…U2n</c>
    /// (dwellings, west to east); bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Bar depth, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The footprint and the zones.</returns>
    public static PlanLayout Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double length = bays * ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, depth), Wing(string.Empty, string.Empty, 0.0, 0.0, length, depth, alongY: false, bays, unitWidth, stairWidth));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The stair bays that fill a wing of length <paramref name="length"/> exactly (ADR-006): <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays, rounded
    /// half away from zero, with the dwelling width adjusted to <c>(L − n·S) / (2n)</c>, which is not positive when the stairs leave no room.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <returns>The bay count and the adjusted dwelling width.</returns>
    public static (int Bays, double UnitWidth) Fit(double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double bays = Math.Max(1.0, Math.Round(length / ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth), MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
        int count = bays >= int.MaxValue ? int.MaxValue : (int)bays;
        return (count, (length - (count * stairWidth)) / (2.0 * count));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The error for a wing whose fitted stair bays leave no dwelling width (<see cref="Fit"/>), or none; the message names the wing, such as
    /// <c>each 17 m wing</c>, and the stairs it would need.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="wing">The wing as named in the message.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or one <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter"/> error.</returns>
    public static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> FitErrors(string wing, double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        (int bays, double width) = Fit(length, unitWidth, stairWidth);
        if (width > 0)
        {
            yield break;
        }

        string need = bays == 1
            ? $"its one stair bay needs {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m of stairs"
            : $"its {bays} stair bays need {Provenance.Format(bays * stairWidth)} m of stairs";
        yield return Diagnostic.Error(
            DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
            $"{nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m leaves no dwelling width in {wing}: {need}; "
            + $"reduce {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} or {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.UnitWidth)}.");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays along a wing that starts at (<paramref name="x0"/>, <paramref name="y0"/>) and runs along +x, or
    /// along +y when <paramref name="alongY"/>; the last bay ends exactly at the wing's length. Zones: <c>{prefix}ST1…</c>, then <c>{prefix}U1…</c>,
    /// numbered along the wing's axis; bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="prefix">Zone ID prefix, such as <c>B-</c>.</param>
    /// <param name="namePrefix">Display name prefix, such as <c>Band </c>.</param>
    /// <param name="x0">Wing start, plan x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="y0">Wing start, plan y, m.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length along its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Wing depth across its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="alongY">Whether the wing runs along +y instead of +x.</param>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The stairs, then the dwellings.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Wing(
        string prefix,
        string namePrefix,
        double x0,
        double y0,
        double length,
        double depth,
        bool alongY,
        int bays,
        double unitWidth,
        double stairWidth)
    {
        double bayWidth = (2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth;
        double start = alongY ? y0 : x0;
        Polygon2 Piece(double from, double to) => alongY ? Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, from, x0 + depth, to) : Polygon2.Rectangle(from, y0, to, y0 + depth);
        var stairs = new List<PlannedZone>();
        var units = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < bays; i++)
        {
            double west = start + (i * bayWidth);
            double stairWest = west + unitWidth;
            double stairEast = stairWest + stairWidth;
            double east = i == bays - 1 ? start + length : west + bayWidth;
            stairs.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}ST{i + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Stair {i + 1}", SpaceType.Stair, Piece(stairWest, stairEast)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(west, stairWest)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 2}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 2}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(stairEast, east)));
        }

        stairs.AddRange(units);
        return stairs;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Generators/WingedBand/WingedBandGenerator.cs` (full file)**

The band and the wings are checked with `StairBayLayout.FitErrors`; the private `WingFits` is removed. The messages are unchanged.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.WingedBand;

/// <summary>Parameters of the winged band (HDHM-TYP-003, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="WingCount">Number of wings projecting from the band; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="WingLength">Length of each wing from the band to its tip, m.</param>
/// <param name="WingSpacing">Clear distance between neighbouring wings along the band, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Depth of the band and of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the band and the wings, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill each exactly.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the band and the wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record WingedBandParameters(
    int WingCount,
    double WingLength,
    double WingSpacing,
    double Depth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 3 wings of 17 m, 17 m apart, 12 m deep, 7 m dwellings and 3 m stairs, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static WingedBandParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 17.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the winged band, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record WingedBandPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Projecting wings joined by a common band (HDHM-TYP-003, ADR-006): a band of stair bays along plan x and <see cref="WingedBandParameters.WingCount"/>
/// wings of stair bays projecting to plan north, the first flush with the band's west end and the last with its east end. Every wing adjoins the band
/// along its whole root (R001), and at least two wings repeat the wing-to-band relation (R002); one wing is rejected (R003, R004). The outline is a
/// comb, so the band's north façade is fragments between the wings' roots and its bay boundaries; ADR-011 governs their windows. Each wing and the band
/// hold <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays with the dwelling width adjusted to fill their length <c>L</c>. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// With <c>D</c> the depth and <c>g</c> the wing spacing, the band is <c>[0, W·D + (W − 1)·g] × [0, D]</c> and wing <c>i</c> (from 1) is
/// <c>[(i − 1)(D + g), (i − 1)(D + g) + D] × [D, D + WingLength]</c>. Zone IDs: <c>B-ST1…</c>, <c>B-U1…</c> (band, west to east), then per wing
/// <c>W{i}-ST1…</c>, <c>W{i}-U1…</c> (south to north); display names <c>Band Unit 3</c>, <c>Wing 2 Stair 1</c>.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class WingedBandGenerator : PlanGenerator<WingedBandParameters, WingedBandPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public WingedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(WingedBandGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(WingedBandParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (parameters.WingCount < 2)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingCount)} must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got {parameters.WingCount}."));
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingLength), parameters.WingLength),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.WingSpacing), parameters.WingSpacing),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(WingedBandParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        errors.AddRange(values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)).Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}.")));
        if (errors.Count == 0)
        {
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"the {Provenance.Format(BandLength(parameters))} m band", BandLength(parameters), parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(parameters.WingLength)} m wing", parameters.WingLength, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(WingedBandParameters p)
    {
        double bandLength = BandLength(p);
        double top = p.Depth + p.WingLength;
        (int bandBays, double bandUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(bandLength, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        (int wingBays, double wingUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(p.WingLength, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>(StairBayLayout.Wing("B-", "Band ", 0.0, 0.0, bandLength, p.Depth, alongY: false, bandBays, bandUnit, p.StairWidth));
        for (int i = 1; i <= p.WingCount; i++)
        {
            zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing($"W{i}-", $"Wing {i} ", WingWest(p, i), p.Depth, p.WingLength, p.Depth, alongY: true, wingBays, wingUnit, p.StairWidth));
        }

        var outline = new List<Point2> { new(0.0, 0.0), new(bandLength, 0.0) };
        for (int i = p.WingCount; i >= 1; i--)
        {
            double west = WingWest(p, i);
            outline.Add(new Point2(west + p.Depth, top));
            outline.Add(new Point2(west, top));
            if (i > 1)
            {
                outline.Add(new Point2(west, p.Depth));
                outline.Add(new Point2(WingWest(p, i - 1) + p.Depth, p.Depth));
            }
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Create(outline).Value, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(WingedBandParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingCount), p.WingCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingLength), Provenance.Format(p.WingLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingSpacing), Provenance.Format(p.WingSpacing)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    private static double BandLength(WingedBandParameters p) => WingWest(p, p.WingCount) + p.Depth;

    private static double WingWest(WingedBandParameters p, int wing) => (wing - 1) * (p.Depth + p.WingSpacing);
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/OpenCourt/OpenCourtGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;

/// <summary>Parameters of the open court (HDHM-TYP-004, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="CourtWidth">Width of the court between the side wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="CourtDepth">Depth of the court from its open side to the north wing, which is also the side wings' length, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Depth of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the wings, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill each wing exactly.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record OpenCourtParameters(
    double CourtWidth,
    double CourtDepth,
    double Depth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: a 24 m × 17 m court, 12 m deep wings, 7 m dwellings and 3 m stairs, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static OpenCourtParameters Default { get; } = new(24.0, 17.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the open court, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record OpenCourtPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Open U-shaped court with stair-served wings (HDHM-TYP-004, ADR-006): three wings of stair bays, west, east, and north, around a court open to plan
/// south. Dwellings lie on three sides of the court (R001), the fourth side stays open (R002), and every dwelling adjoins a stair of its bay (R003). The
/// north wing runs the full width and holds the corners; the side wings run from plan south to it. Each wing holds
/// <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays with the dwelling width adjusted to fill its length <c>L</c>. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// With <c>D</c> the depth, <c>w</c> the court width, and <c>c</c> the court depth: west wing <c>[0, D] × [0, c]</c>, east wing
/// <c>[D + w, 2D + w] × [0, c]</c>, north wing <c>[0, 2D + w] × [c, c + D]</c>. Zone IDs: <c>N-ST1…</c>, <c>N-U1…</c> (north wing, west to east), then
/// <c>W-…</c> and <c>E-…</c> (side wings, south to north); display names <c>North Unit 3</c>, <c>West Stair 1</c>.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class OpenCourtGenerator : PlanGenerator<OpenCourtParameters, OpenCourtPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public OpenCourtGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(OpenCourtGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(OpenCourtParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(OpenCourtParameters.CourtWidth), parameters.CourtWidth),
            (nameof(OpenCourtParameters.CourtDepth), parameters.CourtDepth),
            (nameof(OpenCourtParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(OpenCourtParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(OpenCourtParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        var errors = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (errors.Count == 0)
        {
            double north = NorthLength(parameters);
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"the {Provenance.Format(north)} m north wing", north, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(parameters.CourtDepth)} m side wing", parameters.CourtDepth, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(OpenCourtParameters p)
    {
        double d = p.Depth;
        double east = d + p.CourtWidth;
        double width = NorthLength(p);
        double top = p.CourtDepth + d;
        (int northBays, double northUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(width, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        (int sideBays, double sideUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(p.CourtDepth, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>();
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("N-", "North ", 0.0, p.CourtDepth, width, d, alongY: false, northBays, northUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("W-", "West ", 0.0, 0.0, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("E-", "East ", east, 0.0, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(new[]
        {
            new Point2(0.0, 0.0), new Point2(d, 0.0), new Point2(d, p.CourtDepth), new Point2(east, p.CourtDepth),
            new Point2(east, 0.0), new Point2(east + d, 0.0), new Point2(east + d, top), new Point2(0.0, top),
        }).Value;
        return new PlanLayout(footprint, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(OpenCourtParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtDepth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    private static double NorthLength(OpenCourtParameters p) => p.Depth + p.CourtWidth + p.Depth;
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SimplifierTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsConnectedZonesOfTheSameType()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "Corridor-1", "DwellingUnit-1", "DwellingUnit-2" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Zone south = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1");
        Assert.Equal(160.0, south.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "US1", "US2" }, south.SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value));
        Assert.Equal(5.0, south.Program.Find(LoadType.Lighting, LoadBasis.PerFloorArea)!.Value, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergedZonesLoseTheirSharedPartition()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces, s => s.Zone == s.AdjacentZone);
        Assert.Equal(2, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "DwellingUnit-1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesZonesMeetingAtTJunctionsOnARotatedPlan()
    {
        // The point plate's dwellings meet each other and the core at T-junctions; rotated off the axes, a plain union of their footprints leaves
        // slits where clipping-grid rounding moves a T-junction off its edge, so the zones are united as tiles (ADR-013).
        IGeneratedPlan plate = new PointPlateGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, new PointPlatePresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(plate, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Result<IFloor> floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "DwellingUnit-1" }, floor.Value.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Polygon2 ring = floor.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(4 * 112.0, ring.Area), $"Area {ring.Area}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreHasFourPerimeterZonesAndACore()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "CORE" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)) * (18.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)), floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterSouthHasOneCentredWindowWithTheSouthFacadeGlazing()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan()).Value;

        WallSurface south = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "P-South" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Window window = Assert.Single(south.Windows);
        Assert.Equal(1.2 + 9.0 + 9.0, window.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 9);
        Assert.Equal((3.0 - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 9);
        Assert.Equal(window.Width / 24.0, window.Height / 3.0, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterDepthTooLargeIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 10.0).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreAnActionableError()
    {
        // 8 m deep wings around an open court: the 4.57 m perimeters of a wing's two façades would overlap (ADR-008 precondition).
        IGeneratedPlan court = new OpenCourtGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default with { Depth = 8.0 }, new OpenCourtPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;

        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(court);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core for this footprint: every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0)]
    [InlineData(-1.0)]
    public void NonPositivePerimeterDepthIsAnError(double depth)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, depth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet()
    {
        Polygon2 courtyard = Polygon2.Create(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(30, 0), new Point2(30, 30), new Point2(0, 30) },
            new[] { new[] { new Point2(10, 10), new Point2(20, 10), new Point2(20, 20), new Point2(10, 20) } }).Value;
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(courtyard, 3.0, 0.0, new Zone[0], new Surface[0], Provenance.Of("Test", new KeyValuePair<string, string>[0]));

        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorHasOnlyOutdoorWalls()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(floor.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Mixed, zone.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(6, zone.SourceZones.Count);
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(4, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SetpointsAreFloorAreaWeightedAndRecorded()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Thermostat thermostat = floor.Zones[0].Program.Thermostat!;
        Assert.Equal(21.0, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint[0], 9);
        Assert.Equal(Lod.Core.Aggregation.AggregationMethod.FloorAreaWeighted, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation!.Method);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(new ZoneId("ST"), thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources);
        Assert.Equal(5, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 1)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 2)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 1)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 2)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 4)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 1)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 2)]
    public void EverySimplifierSatisfiesTheInvariantsForRandomPlans(string simplifier, int seed)
    {
        LinearPlanParameters parameters = RandomParameters(new Random(seed));

        IFloor floor = Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan(parameters)).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergingTheUnconditionedStairEnlargesConditionedAreaAndIsReported()
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances)));

        CheckResult conditioned = report.Checks.Single(c => c.Check == "ConditionedFloorArea");
        Assert.False(conditioned.Passed);
        Assert.False(conditioned.Enforced);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAnUncoveredFacade()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Surface[] withoutSouthWall = floor.Surfaces.Where(s => !(s is WallSurface w && w.Start.Y == 0.0 && w.End.Y == 0.0)).ToArray();
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, floor.Zones, withoutSouthWall, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "FacadeCoverage" && !c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.False(report.Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NoSimplificationPassesValidation()
    {
        Assert.True(new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.DetailedFloor()).Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAChangedProgram()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Zone zone = floor.Zones[0];
        var tampered = new Zone(zone.Id, zone.Name, zone.SpaceType, zone.Parts, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, zone.SourceZones);
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, new[] { tampered }, floor.Surfaces, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.False(report.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Installed.Lighting" && !c.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Traceability" && !c.Passed);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void SimplifiersAreDeterministic(string simplifier)
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void CanonicalSnapshots(string simplifier)
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), $"simplifier-{simplifier}");
    }

    private static IPlanSimplifier Simplifier(string name) => name switch
    {
        "SemanticMerge" => new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        "PerimeterCore" => new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth),
        "SingleZonePerFloor" => new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(name), name, "Unknown simplifier."),
    };

    private static LinearPlanParameters RandomParameters(Random random) => new(
        Length: 18.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 42.0),
        UnitDepth: 6.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 4.0),
        CorridorWidth: 1.5 + (random.NextDouble() * 1.5),
        TargetUnitWidth: 5.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 7.0),
        StairLength: 3.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 3.0),
        FloorHeight: 2.8 + (random.NextDouble() * 0.7),
        OrientationDegrees: random.NextDouble() * 360.0);
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (full file)**

Both `PlanTooNarrow` messages of `PerimeterCore` append `Precondition()`; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Simplification;

/// <summary>
/// Z1: merges zones of the same space type that are connected through shared walls. Each connected group becomes one zone, the union of its
/// footprints as tiles (<see cref="PolygonOps.UnionTiles"/>), so zones meeting at T-junctions unite exactly on rotated plans; the union may have
/// holes, where the group surrounds zones of another type.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SemanticMerge : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SemanticMerge);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        Dictionary<ZoneId, Zone> byId = plan.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id);
        ILookup<ZoneId, ZoneId> neighbours = plan.Surfaces
            .Where(s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .ToLookup(s => s.Zone, s => s.AdjacentZone!.Value);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var visited = new HashSet<ZoneId>();
        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        var counters = new Dictionary<SpaceType, int>();
        foreach (Zone seed in plan.Zones.Where(z => !visited.Contains(z.Id)))
        {
            var group = new List<Zone>();
            var queue = new Queue<Zone>(new[] { seed });
            visited.Add(seed.Id);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                Zone current = queue.Dequeue();
                group.Add(current);
                foreach (ZoneId next in neighbours[current.Id].Where(n => !visited.Contains(n) && byId[n].SpaceType == seed.SpaceType))
                {
                    visited.Add(next);
                    queue.Enqueue(byId[next]);
                }
            }

            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> union = ops.UnionTiles(group.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint).ToArray());
            if (union.Count != 1)
            {
                return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.DisconnectedGroup,
                    $"Merged group {string.Join(",", group.Select(z => z.Id.Value))} is not one polygon.",
                    seed.Id.Value));
            }

            int number = counters.TryGetValue(seed.SpaceType, out int n) ? n + 1 : 1;
            counters[seed.SpaceType] = number;
            targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{seed.SpaceType}-{number}"), $"{seed.SpaceType} {number}", seed.SpaceType, union[0]));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}

/// <summary>
/// Z2: perimeter zones of a configurable depth plus a core (ADR-008). Each footprint edge owns the trapezoid between the edge and its inward
/// mitred offset, so corners are split along the bisectors; trapezoids in the same orientation bin are merged. Footprints with holes are not supported yet.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PerimeterCore : PlanSimplifier
{
    private readonly double _depth;

    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="perimeterDepth">Perimeter zone depth, m.</param>
    public PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings tolerances, double perimeterDepth)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
        _depth = perimeterDepth;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(PerimeterCore);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        if (!(_depth > 0) || double.IsInfinity(_depth))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Perimeter depth must be positive, got {_depth}.");
        }

        if (plan.Footprint.Holes.Count > 0)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported, "Perimeter/core of footprints with holes is not supported yet (S8, typologies).");
        }

        IReadOnlyList<Point2> v = plan.Footprint.Outer;
        int n = v.Count;
        Point2[] offset = Enumerable.Range(0, n).Select(i => Mitre(v[(i + n - 1) % n], v[i], v[(i + 1) % n])).ToArray();
        Result<Polygon2> core = Polygon2.Create(offset);
        bool keepsDirections = Enumerable.Range(0, n).All(i =>
        {
            Point2 a = v[i];
            Point2 b = v[(i + 1) % n];
            Point2 c = offset[i];
            Point2 d = offset[(i + 1) % n];
            return ((b.X - a.X) * (d.X - c.X)) + ((b.Y - a.Y) * (d.Y - c.Y)) > 0;
        });
        if (!core.IsSuccess || !keepsDirections || Polygon2.SignedArea(offset) <= 0)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core for this footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var trapezoids = new List<(OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)>();
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            Polygon2 shape = Polygon2.Create(new[] { v[i], v[(i + 1) % n], offset[(i + 1) % n], offset[i] }).Value;
            trapezoids.Add((Orientation.Bin(Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(v[i], v[(i + 1) % n]) + plan.OrientationDegrees), shape));
        }

        double tiled = trapezoids.Sum(t => t.Shape.Area) + core.Value.Area;
        if (!Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, tiled))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter zones and core cover {tiled:0.###} m² of a {plan.Footprint.Area:0.###} m² footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        foreach (IGrouping<OrientationBin, (OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)> bin in trapezoids.GroupBy(t => t.Bin).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
        {
            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> merged = ops.Union(bin.Select(t => t.Shape));
            for (int k = 0; k < merged.Count; k++)
            {
                string suffix = merged.Count == 1 ? string.Empty : $"-{k + 1}";
                targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"P-{bin.Key}{suffix}"), $"Perimeter {bin.Key}{suffix}", SpaceType.Mixed, merged[k]));
            }
        }

        targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId("CORE"), "Core", SpaceType.Mixed, core.Value));
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() =>
        new[] { new KeyValuePair<string, string>("PerimeterDepth", Provenance.Format(_depth)) };

    /// <summary>The precondition a <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow"/> plan breaks, with what the user can do (ADR-008).</summary>
    private string Precondition() =>
        $"every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth ({2.0 * _depth} m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.";

    private Point2 Mitre(Point2 previous, Point2 vertex, Point2 next)
    {
        (double X, double Y) a = InwardNormal(previous, vertex);
        (double X, double Y) b = InwardNormal(vertex, next);
        double scale = _depth / (1.0 + (a.X * b.X) + (a.Y * b.Y));
        return new Point2(vertex.X + ((a.X + b.X) * scale), vertex.Y + ((a.Y + b.Y) * scale));
    }

    private static (double X, double Y) InwardNormal(Point2 from, Point2 to)
    {
        double length = from.DistanceTo(to);
        return (-(to.Y - from.Y) / length, (to.X - from.X) / length);
    }

    private static Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> Fail(string code, string message) =>
        Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(code, message));
}

/// <summary>Z3: the whole floor as one zone.</summary>
public sealed class SingleZonePerFloor : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SingleZonePerFloor(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SingleZonePerFloor);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        SpaceType[] types = plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct().ToArray();
        SpaceType type = types.Length == 1 ? types[0] : SpaceType.Mixed;
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(new[] { new TargetZone(new ZoneId("FLOOR"), "Floor", type, plan.Footprint) });
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}
```

```markdown
- A plan narrower than `2d` fails: the canonical plan with `d` = 10 m returns `PlanTooNarrow`. The user chooses a smaller depth; the simplifier never chooses one.
```

   with

```markdown
- A plan narrower than `2d` fails: the canonical plan with `d` = 10 m returns `PlanTooNarrow`. The user chooses a smaller depth; the simplifier never chooses one.
- Non-rectangular plans (S8.2, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)): the bisector rule holds unchanged at re-entrant corners, where the two trapezoids meet on the diagonal of the inside corner, so the comb of the winged band and the U of the open court pass the checks at their default 12 m wing depth with the default 4.57 m depth, and a façade direction with several runs (the court's three south-facing façades) gives numbered zones such as `P-South-1`. Every wing, plate, and other part of the footprint must be wider than `2d`: the open court with 8 m wings fails with `PlanTooNarrow`, whose message states this precondition and suggests a smaller depth or another simplifier. Supporting narrower wings would need a different partition, such as the straight skeleton (option 3), and a new ADR.
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/NonRectangularTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

151 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (48), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (96), `SemanticMergeJoinsOnlyConnectedZonesOfOneType` (3: 2 zones for the point plate, 13 for the winged band, 9 for the open court), `ThePointPlateDwellingsMergeIntoARingAroundTheCore` (1), and `PerimeterCoreGivesEachFacadeRunItsOwnZone` (3: `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, `CORE` for the point plate; 13 zones for the winged band; 9 for the open court).

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>The S8.2 families without holes (ADR-006): the point plate, the winged band, and the open court through every simplifier and aggregator.</summary>
public sealed class NonRectangularTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Families = { "PointPlate", "WingedBand", "OpenCourt" };
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from family in Families
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { family, simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string family, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(family), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string family, string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(family), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("PointPlate", "DwellingUnit-1,Stair-1")]
    [InlineData("WingedBand", "DwellingUnit-1,DwellingUnit-2,DwellingUnit-3,DwellingUnit-4,DwellingUnit-5,DwellingUnit-6,Stair-1,Stair-2,Stair-3,Stair-4,Stair-5,Stair-6,Stair-7")]
    [InlineData("OpenCourt", "DwellingUnit-1,DwellingUnit-2,DwellingUnit-3,DwellingUnit-4,Stair-1,Stair-2,Stair-3,Stair-4,Stair-5")]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsOnlyConnectedZonesOfOneType(string family, string zones)
    {
        // A wing's root dwelling joins the band or north-wing dwellings it adjoins; stairs never adjoin each other and stay apart.
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(family)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePointPlateDwellingsMergeIntoARingAroundTheCore()
    {
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan("PointPlate")).Value;

        Polygon2 ring = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(22.0 * 22.0, ring.Area + floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "Stair-1").FloorArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(8, 14), new Point2(14, 14), new Point2(14, 8), new Point2(8, 8) }.OrderBy(p => p.X).ThenBy(p => p.Y), Assert.Single(ring.Holes).OrderBy(p => p.X).ThenBy(p => p.Y));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "Stair-1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Assert.Equal(4, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "DwellingUnit-1" && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "Stair-1"));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("PointPlate", "P-North,P-East,P-South,P-West,CORE")]
    [InlineData("WingedBand", "P-North-1,P-North-2,P-North-3,P-North-4,P-North-5,P-East-1,P-East-2,P-East-3,P-South,P-West-1,P-West-2,P-West-3,CORE")]
    [InlineData("OpenCourt", "P-North,P-East-1,P-East-2,P-South-1,P-South-2,P-South-3,P-West-1,P-West-2,CORE")]
    public void PerimeterCoreGivesEachFacadeRunItsOwnZone(string family, string zones)
    {
        // Bisector trapezoids also at the re-entrant corners (ADR-008); a direction with several separate façade runs gives numbered zones.
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan(family)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(Plan(family).Footprint.Area, floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea)));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    /// <summary>The canonical plan of a family: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(string family)
    {
        ProgramPreset unit = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit;
        ProgramPreset stair = ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair;
        return family switch
        {
            "PointPlate" => new PointPlateGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, new PointPlatePresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            "WingedBand" => new WingedBandGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(WingedBandParameters.Default, new WingedBandPresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            "OpenCourt" => new OpenCourtGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default, new OpenCourtPresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(family), family, "Unknown family."),
        };
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output.
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}

public sealed class TerraceRowComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public TerraceRowComponent()
        : base(
            "Terrace Row",
            "Terr",
            "Attached narrow terrace row (HDHM-TYP-006, ADR-006): a row of attached house plots sharing dividing walls, one dwelling zone per plot and storey; each house's own stair is inside the dwelling and not zoned.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("f694e595-dba2-4b98-9d1e-f1492e3edf39");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        TerraceRowParameters d = TerraceRowParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Plot Count", "PC", "Number of attached house plots along the row; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.PlotCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Plot Width", "PW", "Width of each plot along the row, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.PlotWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Row depth, which every house spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the houses");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int plots = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref plots) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 4, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 5) is not { } dwellingUnit)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new TerraceRowParameters(plots, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3]);
        Output(DA, new TerraceRowGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new TerraceRowPresets(dwellingUnit)));
    }
}

public sealed class PointPlateComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public PointPlateComponent()
        : base(
            "Point Plate",
            "PPlate",
            "Compact point-access tower plate (HDHM-TYP-007, ADR-006): a square plate with one central core, a Stair zone without outdoor walls, and four dwellings in a pinwheel around it, each adjoining the core and with two façades. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("8ac9044a-8fbe-4964-a9de-2ef8be35094a");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        PointPlateParameters d = PointPlateParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Dwelling Depth", "DD", "Depth of each dwelling from its façade to the core, m; the plate side is Core Width + 2 × Dwelling Depth.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.DwellingDepth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Core Width", "CW", "Side of the square core, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.CoreWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the four dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the core");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        if (!TryNumbers(DA, 0, 4, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 4) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 5) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new PointPlateParameters(v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3]);
        Output(DA, new PointPlateGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new PointPlatePresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}

public sealed class WingedBandComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public WingedBandComponent()
        : base(
            "Winged Band",
            "WBand",
            "Projecting wings joined by a common band (HDHM-TYP-003, ADR-006): a band of stair bays with wings of stair bays projecting to plan north, each joined to the band along its whole root; the comb outline gives the band short façade fragments between the wings. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("e285811d-2fdb-4d31-8b02-62c270b609cf");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        WingedBandParameters d = WingedBandParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Wing Count", "WC", "Number of wings projecting from the band; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.WingCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Wing Length", "WL", "Length of each wing from the band to its tip, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.WingLength);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Wing Spacing", "WS", "Clear distance between neighbouring wings along the band, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.WingSpacing);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Depth of the band and of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Target dwelling width, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill the band and each wing exactly.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the band and the wings, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int wings = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref wings) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 7, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 8) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 9) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new WingedBandParameters(wings, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4], v[5], v[6]);
        Output(DA, new WingedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new WingedBandPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}
```
