The three motifs are added after `terrace_row`, and the three icon entries after *Terrace Row* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "PointPlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(point_plate)),
    "WingedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(winged_band)),
    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```markdown
A **stair bay** is dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each spanning the full bar depth: the stair serves the two dwellings beside it on every storey, and every dwelling has two opposite outdoor façades. `StairBayLayout` (in `Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/`, internal) lays out *n* bays from given widths, along plan x from the origin or, for a wing, from any start along plan x or plan y (`Wing`), and fits the bays to a wing's length (`Fit`, `FitErrors`); `HDHM-TYP-001`, `HDHM-TYP-008`, the wings of 003 and 004, and later 005 use it, so the bay geometry exists once. In a bay of width `2·UnitWidth + StairWidth`, the west dwelling is `[x₀, x₀ + U]`, the stair `[x₀ + U, x₀ + U + S]`, and the east dwelling `[x₀ + U + S, x₀ + 2U + S]`.

Validation additionally requires that adjusted width to be positive: stairs that leave no room for dwellings are `InvalidParameter`, for example `StairWidth 10 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m wing: its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.` Within a wing, zones are listed stairs first, then dwellings, numbered along the wing's axis, and each wing's last bay ends exactly at the wing's end.

### Pipeline consequences of S8.2

Running every plan simplifier and floor aggregator on the non-rectangular families, canonical and rotated, found three faults on rotated plans that also affected the S8.1 bars and the linear plan at some placements; each is fixed and documented where it belongs, and no tolerance or check is relaxed:

- *Semantic Merge* united its groups with a plain union, which splits a group whose zones meet at T-junctions once rotated (`DisconnectedGroup`); it now unites them as tiles ([ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)).
- The surface builder tested whether a vertex lies on an edge between snapped grid cells, which can put a T-junction exactly on an edge up to √2 grid units off it (`UnmatchedEdge`); it now measures the original positions against the distance tolerance (ADR-002).
- A union of tiles returned vertices rounded to the clipping grid, so a storey outline united from a *Semantic Merge* floor could lie off the merged walls' façade lines (`FacadeNotCovered` in the single-zone aggregators); it now keeps the tiles' own vertices (ADR-002, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)).

The bisector rule of *Perimeter Core* needed no change: it handles the re-entrant corners of the comb and the U. Its precondition, that every wing or other part of the footprint be wider than twice the perimeter depth, is stated in its `PlanTooNarrow` message and in [ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md); with the defaults (12 m deep wings and plates of 22 m, 4.57 m depth) every family passes.
```

```markdown
- The source's abstract validation records are not used as tests (D-083); a sub-stage that refines a layout updates the realisation text here.

5. Replace

```markdown
- Surfaces come from `LayoutSurfaceBuilder`: an edge shared by two zones becomes an interzone wall, an edge on the plan boundary an outdoor wall; any other edge (a gap or an overlap) is an error. Floors and ceilings stay `Unresolved` until floors are aggregated.
```

   with

```markdown
- Point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`): a square plate of side `CoreWidth + 2 · DwellingDepth` (22 m by default) with a central core `ST` (*Core*, a `Stair` zone, 6 m square) and four dwellings `U1…U4` of `DwellingDepth` 8 m in a pinwheel around it, counter-clockwise from the south-west; every dwelling adjoins the core along one of the core's sides and has two façades; the core has no outdoor wall, the first zone of any family without one.
- Winged band (`HDHM-TYP-003`): a band of stair bays along plan x and `WingCount` (≥ 2, default 3) wings of stair bays projecting to plan north, `WingLength` 17 m long and `WingSpacing` 17 m apart, all `Depth` 12 m deep; the first wing is flush with the band's west end, the last with its east end, so the outline is a comb. Every wing adjoins the band along its whole root. The band and each wing hold `n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))` bays with the dwelling width adjusted to fill their length `L` (the default 70 m band has four bays of 7.25 m dwellings, the wings one bay each); stairs that leave no dwelling width are the error `InvalidParameter`. Zones `B-ST1…`, `B-U1…` (band, west to east), then `W{i}-ST1…`, `W{i}-U1…` (wing `i`, south to north). The band's north façade is fragments between the wings' roots and its bay boundaries (1.25 m to 7.25 m by default); a fragment shorter than 1 m gets no window (`WindowOmitted`, ADR-011).
- Open court (`HDHM-TYP-004`): three wings of stair bays, `Depth` 12 m deep, around a court `CourtWidth` 24 m wide and `CourtDepth` 17 m deep that is open to plan south: west and east wings of length `CourtDepth` and a north wing across the full width `2 · Depth + CourtWidth` that holds the corners. Bays are fitted to each wing as in the winged band. Zones `N-…` (west to east), then `W-…` and `E-…` (south to north); every dwelling adjoins a stair of its bay.
- Surfaces come from `LayoutSurfaceBuilder`: an edge shared by two zones becomes an interzone wall, an edge on the plan boundary an outdoor wall; any other edge (a gap or an overlap) is an error. Whether a vertex lies on an edge, to split it there or to place it on the boundary, is measured between the vertices' original positions against the distance tolerance, not between their snapped grid cells, so T-junctions are recognised on rotated plans (ADR-002, S8.2). Floors and ceilings stay `Unresolved` until floors are aggregated.
```

6. Replace

```markdown
- *Semantic Merge* merges connected zones of the same space type. *Perimeter Core* makes one perimeter zone per orientation plus a core, with corners split on the bisectors (ADR-008). *Single Zone per Floor* makes the floor one zone.
```

   with

```markdown
- *Semantic Merge* merges connected zones of the same space type; each group's footprint is the union of its zones as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), which keeps the zones' own vertices and may have holes: the four dwellings of the point plate become one ring around the core (ADR-002). *Perimeter Core* makes one perimeter zone per orientation plus a core, with corners split on the bisectors (ADR-008), also at re-entrant corners; an orientation with several separate façade runs, such as the open court's three south façades, gives the zones `P-South-1…`. Every wing or other part of the footprint must be wider than twice the depth, or the simplifier fails with `PlanTooNarrow`, whose message says so. *Single Zone per Floor* makes the floor one zone.
```

7. Replace

```markdown
The single-zone methods share one merge (`SingleZoneMerge`); their storey outlines are unions of the zone footprints as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), so rotated plans give clean outlines. Programs are aggregated per zone with `EquivalentPropertyAggregator` from the stacked zones (fractions 1), so every invariant is conserved; a zone is conditioned if any source is (D-038). `SingleZoneBuilding` is the former `SingleZoneMerged`, and `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` the former `FloorAreaMultiplier` (D-071).
```

   with

```markdown
The single-zone methods share one merge (`SingleZoneMerge`); their storey outlines are unions of the zone footprints as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), so rotated plans give clean outlines whose vertices are the zones' own vertices (S8.2). Programs are aggregated per zone with `EquivalentPropertyAggregator` from the stacked zones (fractions 1), so every invariant is conserved; a zone is conditioned if any source is (D-038). `SingleZoneBuilding` is the former `SingleZoneMerged`, and `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` the former `FloorAreaMultiplier` (D-071).
```

Append this section after the last line of the file (after the S8.1 checklist), separated by one blank line, so that it nests under `## Stage checklists` like the S2–S8.1 checklists:

```markdown
### S8.2 checklist — non-rectangular typologies (`v0.8.1`)

S8.2 adds three plan generator components in *2 Generate* for the families without holes ([ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) and makes *Semantic Merge* and the single-zone aggregators exact on rotated plans ([ADR-002](../decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md)). No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes. Use *Dwelling Unit Preset* and *Stair Preset* with nothing connected.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *2 Generate* shows *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court* next to the S8.1 generators, each with its own green icon (a square pinwheel around a dark core, a comb, and a U).
- [ ] Inputs, in order, with their defaults: *Point Plate* (PPlate) Dwelling Depth 8, Core Width 6, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Winged Band* (WBand) Wing Count 3, Wing Length 17, Wing Spacing 17, Depth 12, Unit Width 7, Stair Width 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Open Court* (OCourt) Court Width 24, Court Depth 17, Depth 12, Unit Width 7, Stair Width 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location. Each has one output, *Plan*.
- [ ] With a preset input unconnected, the component shows Grasshopper's missing-input warning and outputs nothing (D-065).

#### Default plans

1. Place each new generator with its presets connected and nothing else changed; connect *Inspect* to each *Plan*.
   - [ ] *Point Plate*: no runtime message; five zones `ST` (*Core*), `U1`–`U4`; footprint 22 m × 22 m (484 m²), core 36 m² at x, y = 8–14 m with no outdoor wall, dwellings 112 m² in a pinwheel; 8 windows, 79.2 m² of glazing (19.8 m² per orientation in *Validate* on its *No Simplification* floor).
   - [ ] *Winged Band*: no runtime message; 21 zones `B-ST1`–`B-ST4`, `B-U1`–`B-U8`, `W1-ST1`, `W1-U1`, `W1-U2`, … `W3-U2`; a comb in a 70 m × 29 m box (1452 m²) with three 12 m wings to plan north; 43 windows, 217.8 m² of glazing (59.4 north, 55.8 south, 51.3 east, 51.3 west); the band's north façade between the wings carries a window on every fragment, the shortest 1.25 m.
   - [ ] *Open Court*: no runtime message; 15 zones `N-ST1`–`N-ST3`, `N-U1`–`N-U6`, `W-ST1`, `W-U1`, `W-U2`, `E-ST1`, `E-U1`, `E-U2`; a U in a 48 m × 29 m box (984 m²) whose 24 m × 17 m court is open to plan south; 30 windows, 154.8 m² of glazing (41.4 south, 37.8 north, 37.8 east, 37.8 west).
   - [ ] Each *Inspect* report equals the snapshot `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/<Family>/Snapshots/<family>-canonical.txt`.

#### Combinations

2. Connect each new plan to the four plan simplifiers (*Perimeter Core* Depth 4.57) and each floor three times, Multipliers 1, 3, 1, to the four floor aggregators.
   - [ ] *Semantic Merge* gives 2 zones for *Point Plate* (`DwellingUnit-1`, a ring around the core, previewed with the hole, and `Stair-1`), 13 for *Winged Band*, and 9 for *Open Court*.
   - [ ] *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, `CORE` for *Point Plate*; 13 zones for *Winged Band* (`P-North-1`–`P-North-5`, `P-East-1`–`P-East-3`, `P-South`, `P-West-1`–`P-West-3`, `CORE`) and 9 for *Open Court* (`P-North`, `P-East-1`, `P-East-2`, `P-South-1`–`P-South-3`, `P-West-1`, `P-West-2`, `CORE`), with diagonal walls also at the re-entrant corners.
   - [ ] For all 16 combinations of each generator, *Validate* gives Passed = True (only `ConditionedFloorArea` may read `note`), no aggregator shows a warning, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False converts without an error.

#### Rotated placement

3. Put each new plan through *Transform Plan* with a 30° rotation about the world z axis and a move by 5.3, −2.1, 0, then through each simplifier and each aggregator (Multipliers 1, 3, 1); repeat with a 131.4° rotation and a move by 15.08, −153.9, 0.
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True for all 16 combinations of each generator and placement, with the same total glazing as the unrotated plan.
   - [ ] *Gallery Bar* (S8.1) through *Transform Plan* with a 321.2° rotation and a move by 136.4, −118.8, 0, then *Semantic Merge*, then *Single Zone Building* and *Single Zone per Floor Type*: no error and *Validate* passes (a regression check of the S8.2 geometry fixes; the integration test `SingleZoneMethodsCoverTheFacadesOfARotatedSemanticMergeFloor` covers the same case).

#### Parameters, preconditions, and the minimum geometry

4. Change inputs one at a time and set each back afterwards.
   - [ ] *Winged Band* Wing Count 1: `Error InvalidParameter: WingCount must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got 1.` and no plan. A length of 0 on any of the three generators gives `… must be positive, got 0.`, for example *Point Plate* Core Width 0: `Error InvalidParameter: CoreWidth must be positive, got 0.`
   - [ ] *Open Court* Unit Width 0.5 and Stair Width 20: `Error InvalidParameter: StairWidth 20 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m side wing: its one stair bay needs 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.`
   - [ ] *Winged Band* Wing Spacing 14: the plan is produced with two remarks, `Info WindowOmitted [B-U4/W4]: Outdoor wall B-U4/W4 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.45 m² of glazing not placed).` and the same for `B-U5/W3`; those two fragments show no window.
   - [ ] *Open Court* Depth 8 through *Perimeter Core* (Depth 4.57): `Error PlanTooNarrow: Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core for this footprint: every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.` and no floor; the other three simplifiers still work.
```

Load the plugin as `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` describes and tick every item of the S8.2 checklist. In this run (D-084, under the rules of D-066) the controller runs the checks that do not need a viewport as a scripted headless Grasshopper session (D-056: Rhino 8 `/nosplash /netcore` with `RHINO_PACKAGE_DIRS` pointing at `src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/Release/net7.0/`, components placed by GUID, states, messages, outputs, data trees, bounding boxes, and the *Boundaries* and *Windows* outputs of *Convert2BEM* written to a file). The following values are expected; they were observed in such a session on the verified reference (Rhino 8.25.25314.11001, .NET 8.0.31; the reference assembly reported the informational version `0.5.0+<sha>`, because the reference keeps the S5 version number; on this branch *BEMGen Info* shows `0.8.0+<sha>`, and the bump to 0.8.1 comes in Task 15). "The presets" are the default *Dwelling Unit Preset* and *Stair Preset* with nothing connected; "1, 3, 1" means a simplified floor given three times to an aggregator with those multipliers.

1. **Registration.** 28 components and 6 parameters, each with a 24 × 24 icon, plus a 24 × 24 assembly icon. In *2 Generate*, all primary: *Gallery Bar* GBar `a58df462-b3c2-46b7-be3d-7ea9f4629280`, *Linear Plan Generator* Linear `a4a9131e-0a0f-49e0-99a4-f26760f6810f`, *Open Court* OCourt `223014ea-3c01-4d2b-b8ba-92ebdc4a7327`, *Point Plate* PPlate `8ac9044a-8fbe-4964-a9de-2ef8be35094a`, *Stair Bay Bar* SBar `911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3`, *Stair Pair* SPair `5669d39b-45e6-47ea-9250-f2304d005335`, *Terrace Row* Terr `f694e595-dba2-4b98-9d1e-f1492e3edf39`, *Transform Plan* TPlan `57c17b9c-04b4-4595-9150-bb923877d95e`, *Winged Band* WBand `e285811d-2fdb-4d31-8b02-62c270b609cf`. Every other component and parameter as in S8.1.
2. **Inputs.** In order, with their persistent defaults (model in metres): *Point Plate* Dwelling Depth (DD) 8, Core Width (CW) 6, Floor Height (FH) 3, Orientation (O) 0, Dwelling Unit (DU), Stair (St), Preview Location (PL, optional) `{0,0,0}`; *Winged Band* Wing Count (WC, integer) 3, Wing Length (WL) 17, Wing Spacing (WS) 17, Depth (D) 12, Unit Width (UW) 7, Stair Width (SW) 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Open Court* Court Width (CW) 24, Court Depth (CD) 17, Depth (D) 12, Unit Width (UW) 7, Stair Width (SW) 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location. Every input except *Preview Location* is required. With the preset inputs unconnected each component is in the warning state with `Input parameter DU failed to collect data` and `Input parameter St failed to collect data` and outputs nothing.
3. **Defaults.** Each new generator with the presets → *Inspect*; → *No Simplification* → *Stack Floors* (one floor) → *Convert2BEM*; and → *Semantic Merge*, *Perimeter Core* (default depth), and *Single Zone per Floor*: no runtime message on the generator, *Inspect*, a simplifier, *Stack Floors*, or *Convert2BEM* (the preset components show only their remark).

   | Generator | Plan bounding box (m) | `footprint area=` | *Boundaries* | *Windows* (Breps in branches) | Floors of *Semantic Merge*, *Perimeter Core*, *Single Zone per Floor* |
   | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
   | *Point Plate* | (0, 0, 0)–(22, 22, 3) | 484.000000 | Adiabatic 16, Ground 5, Outdoors 13 | 8 in 5 (0, 2, 2, 2, 2) | 2, 5, 1 zones |
   | *Winged Band* | (0, 0, 0)–(70, 29, 3) | 1452.000000 | Adiabatic 50, Ground 21, Outdoors 64 | 43 in 21 (1, 2, 2, 1, then 2 in each of branches 4–13, then 3, 2, 2, 3, 2, 2, 3) | 13, 13, 1 zones |
   | *Open Court* | (0, 0, 0)–(48, 29, 3) | 984.000000 | Adiabatic 36, Ground 15, Outdoors 45 | 30 in 15 (1, 2, 1, then 2 in each of branches 3–9, then 3, 2, 2, 3, 2) | 9, 9, 1 zones |

4. **Every combination.** Each new generator's plan through each of the four plan simplifiers (*Perimeter Core* with its default depth), each floor given 1, 3, 1 to each of the four aggregators, each building to *Validate*: no runtime message on any simplifier, aggregator, or *Validate*, and *Validate* Passed = `True` for all 48 buildings.
5. **Rotated plan.** Each new plan through *Transform Plan* with the transform of a 30° rotation about the world z axis followed by a move by (5.3, −2.1, 0), then the 16 combinations of item 4; and again with a 131.4° rotation and a move by (15.08, −153.9, 0): no runtime message (including *Transform Plan*) and *Validate* Passed = `True` for all 96.
6. **Regression of the S8.2 fixes.** *Gallery Bar* with the presets through *Transform Plan* (321.2°, then a move by (136.4, −118.8, 0)) → *Semantic Merge* (three times) → *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* (1, 3, 1) → *Validate*: no runtime message and Passed = `True` for both.
7. **Parameters, preconditions, and the minimum geometry.** *Winged Band* with Wing Count 1: error state, `Error InvalidParameter: WingCount must be at least 2 (the wing-to-band relation repeats), got 1.`, no plan. *Point Plate* with Core Width 0: error state, `Error InvalidParameter: CoreWidth must be positive, got 0.` *Open Court* with Unit Width 0.5 and Stair Width 20: error state, `Error InvalidParameter: StairWidth 20 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m side wing: its one stair bay needs 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.` *Winged Band* with Wing Spacing 14: remark state with the two remarks `Info WindowOmitted [B-U4/W4]: Outdoor wall B-U4/W4 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.45 m² of glazing not placed).` and the same for `B-U5/W3`, and a plan. *Open Court* with Depth 8: the generator in the remark state with the two remarks `Info WindowOmitted [N-U1/W2]: Outdoor wall N-U1/W2 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.45 m² of glazing not placed).` and the same for `N-U4/W1`, and a plan; *Perimeter Core* on it in the error state with `Error PlanTooNarrow: Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core for this footprint: every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.`

Items that a headless session cannot observe stay open for a person (D-056, D-058): the toolbar and canvas icons, the viewport previews (the point plate's *Semantic Merge* ring with its hole, the diagonal walls of *Perimeter Core* at the re-entrant corners, the fragments without a window), the *Inspect* reports compared with the three canonical snapshots (the generator tests check the snapshots), the zone IDs of *Semantic Merge* and *Perimeter Core* (`NonRectangularTypologyTests` checks them; the headless session recorded the zone counts), the glazing values of *Validate* and the totals of the rotated plans (only Passed was recorded; the generator tests check the glazing of every wall), *Convert2BEM* on the 16 combinations (the headless session converted the one-storey default plans only), and the other three simplifiers on the 8 m open court. No example definition is part of S8.2; the pending S4 definition (`examples/grasshopper/checkpoint-1-pipeline.gh`) stays person-only (D-058).

```markdown
Checkpoint 1 (D-030) is reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), and so are the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`, D-064) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`, D-073), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`, D-079), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`) and S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`, D-084). The pipeline runs end to end for eight plan generators: program presets → a plan generator (one `PlanGenerator` subclass per *High-Density Housing* typology family with typed parameter and preset records, [ADR-006](docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md): `LinearPlanGenerator`, the double-sided internal corridor bar `HDHM-TYP-009`; the bar families `StairBayBarGenerator` 001, `StairPairGenerator` 008, `GalleryBarGenerator` 002 with its gallery not modelled, and `TerraceRowGenerator` 006 with one dwelling zone per plot and storey, D-085; and the non-rectangular families `PointPlateGenerator` 007, a square plate whose core has no outdoor wall, `WingedBandGenerator` 003, a comb, and `OpenCourtGenerator` 004, a U; one typed preset per space type, D-061; optionally rotated and moved by `PlanTransform`, D-070) → plan simplifiers (`NoSimplification`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`) → floor aggregators (`Stack`, `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`, D-071; floors and ceilings designated on the assembled stack, D-068) → validation → `Convert2BEM` (heat transfer between zones an export option, off by default, D-069), as described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md). Every generator places one centred window per outdoor wall (D-026), except on a wall shorter than 1 m or where the window would be narrower or lower than 0.3 m (`GeometryLimits`, info `WindowOmitted`, [ADR-011](docs/decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt (`SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`), every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (`CentredWindows`, D-079, [ADR-012](docs/decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)); the other steps keep their input's windows. Merged zones are unions of their sources as tiles that keep the sources' own vertices, so they may have holes and stay exact on rotated plans, and vertices on edges are recognised at their original positions (D-088, [ADR-002](docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md)); `PerimeterCore` needs every part of a footprint to be wider than twice its depth and otherwise fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087, [ADR-008](docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)). Vertical aggregation follows [ADR-013](docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md), which supersedes [ADR-009](docs/decisions/ADR-009-vertical-aggregation.md) where it differs. ADR-006, ADR-011, ADR-012, ADR-013, and the S4.2, S5, S8.1, and S8.2 plans were accepted by the controller and await the owner's reading (D-075, D-080, D-084). In Grasshopper every component has an icon drawn by `scripts/make_icons.py` (D-063), and the generator, *Transform Plan*, simplifier, and aggregator components have a *Preview Location* input that moves only their preview (D-062). Until the owner says otherwise, changes need not keep backward compatibility, but component GUIDs never change (D-064).

Not implemented yet: `Lod.Export` and Convert2IDF (S6), the enclosed courtyard 005 with footprints with holes (S8.3), and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The precedent study of S7 was carried out in a separate repository (D-082); its nine families are paraphrased in [docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md). The example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values, and the `Convert2BEM` output layout stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023).
```

Checkpoint 1 reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), followed by the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`) and S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`). In Rhino 8 the plugin runs the whole pipeline for eight plan generators: program presets with conditioning (a default preset component per space type); the linear plan generator and seven typologies of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study (ADR-006), the bars *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, and *Terrace Row* and the non-rectangular *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court*, each with one preset input per space type and explicit windows (none on a wall shorter than 1 m or for a window below 0.3 m, ADR-011); *Transform Plan* to rotate and move a plan; four plan simplifiers (Z0–Z3); four floor aggregators (Stack, StackedFloorZoneMultiplier, SingleZonePerFloorType, SingleZoneBuilding) that designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack and warn about unsupported storeys; invariant validation; and `Convert2BEM`, which writes heat transfer between zones only when asked (off by default). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt, every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the windows it covers, so glazing is conserved per wall, façade, orientation, and building (D-079); merged zones stay exact on rotated plans (D-088). Every component has an icon, and every component with a viewport preview has a *Preview Location* input that moves only its preview. The pipeline is described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md); the example definition `examples/grasshopper/checkpoint-1-pipeline.gh`, which wires every simplifier into every aggregator, is still to be built by a person. Next: Convert2IDF (S6) and the enclosed courtyard (S8.3). The `Convert2BEM` output stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023), and the example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values.
```

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-084) · **Status:** Provisional

Each fix repairs rotated plans that already failed on `v0.8.0` (the linear plan and the S8.1 bars through *Semantic Merge*). D-087: *Perimeter Core*'s `PlanTooNarrow` message states that every part of the footprint must be wider than twice the perimeter depth ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)); its checks are unchanged. No tolerance or validation check changed.
- The twelve existing snapshots are byte-identical; three canonical snapshots are new. 154 core, 159 generator, and 474 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including every new family with every simplifier and aggregator on its canonical plan and on the plan rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m.

## Exit criteria

- `PointPlateGenerator` (007, the core `ST` without an outdoor wall and four dwellings in a pinwheel), `WingedBandGenerator` (003, a comb of a band and at least two wings), and `OpenCourtGenerator` (004, a U open to plan south) implement ADR-006 with its defaults, zone IDs, and validation; each has tiling, window, relationship, invalid-parameter, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests; `StairBayLayout` lays out the wings (`Wing`, `Fit`, `FitErrors`) and the S8.1 bars unchanged.
- *Semantic Merge* unites its groups as tiles, the surface builder tests vertices on edges at their original positions against the unchanged distance tolerance, and unions of tiles keep the tiles' own vertices (D-088); each fix has a test that failed on the code before it (`SimplifierTests`, `LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests`, `GeometryTests`, `AggregatorTests`), and ADR-002 and ADR-013 describe the rules.
- *Perimeter Core*'s `PlanTooNarrow` message states the width precondition (D-087), tested by `SimplifierTests`; ADR-008 records it.
- Every new family validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plan and at two rotated and moved placements (`NonRectangularTypologyTests`, 151 cases).
- Snapshots: the twelve existing snapshots byte-identical; three new canonical snapshots.
- Three new components in *2 Generate* with the GUIDs of this plan, their ADR-006 defaults, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last, and icons (35 icons); no existing component, input, output, or GUID changed.
- ADR-006 records the S8.2 families as implemented and the pipeline consequences; `pipeline.md`, the precedent page, and `program-presets.md` describe S8.2; the S8.2 smoke-test checklist is in the guide.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 154 core, 159 generators, 474 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- The S8.2 smoke test is recorded in the close-out commit, with the items left for a person named.
- Version 0.8.1, local tag `v0.8.1`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line and detailed-plans row, and the decision-log entry D-089 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- Nothing is pushed in this run (D-084); Task 16 is left for the owner.

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and documentation replacement was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on the S8.1 reference (`d4aae8e`), 12 commits ending at `09c0ac3`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings. Local `main` is `1794af1`: S8.1 executed (`v0.8.0`, `4e89b4e`; `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` identical to the S8.1 reference, `Directory.Build.props` at 0.8.0) plus the entries D-087 and D-088, so every file the reference touches is identical on both. The reference's commit order is kept with one exception: its component commit is split into Task 9 (the components) and Task 10 (the icons and `make_icons.py`), as S8.1 did with its Tasks 11 and 13. The reference has no roadmap commit; the roadmap edits are the plan commit's and the close-out's. Every task was then replayed on a clone of `1794af1`, taking each file content and each replacement from this plan's text: the red errors of Tasks 1, 5, and 6 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the failing tests and messages of Tasks 2, 3, 4, and 7, the single failing snapshot test of Tasks 1, 5, and 6 and the three received snapshots (byte-identical to the expected contents), the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 10 and the 35 hashes after it (all 35 PNGs byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied, and the result equals the reference), the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on a fresh clone of the replayed `v0.8.1` (154 core, 159 generators, 474 integration) are observed results. The replayed end state equals the reference apart from line endings, the version (the reference keeps 0.5.0), and the files the reference holds in their S8.1 form or not at all (the decision log, the roadmap, AGENTS.md, README.md, the plans, and the `.gitignore` lines `main` gained in S8.1).
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.2; this plan uses five slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `feature/generators-point-plate`, `fix/geometry-rotated-plans` (the three D-088 fixes, type `fix`), `feature/generators-non-rectangular-typologies` (which also holds the `fix(simplifiers)` commit of Task 7, because its test needs the open court of Task 6), `feature/grasshopper-non-rectangular-typologies`, `docs/architecture-non-rectangular-typologies`, and `chore/repo-s8.2-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. Like S8.1, this run ends without a push (D-084); Task 16 is written out for the owner, pushes both `v0.8.0` and `v0.8.1`, and stays unticked.
3. **D-087, D-088, and the reference documents.** D-087 and D-088 were logged on `main` after the reference's documents were settled. The rules are the same, and D-088's "Details in ADR-002 and ADR-013" is where Tasks 2–4 put them, but the reference texts of ADR-002, ADR-006, ADR-008, and ADR-013 do not cite D-087 or D-088. The plan keeps the reference text unchanged; the plan commit's roadmap bullet, the close-out's AGENTS.md, README.md, roadmap Progress line, and D-089 cite them. Citing the two entries in the ADRs is a one-line follow-up for the owner's reading.
4. **The fixes repair behaviour released up to `v0.8.0`.** On the code of `1794af1` (that is, `v0.8.0`), the reference build's sweep probe (not committed: seed 12345, four fixed and 40 random rotations and moves, the linear plan and the four S8.1 bars, every simplifier with every aggregator, floors 1, 3, 1) gave `total 3520, failing 16`, every failure in *Semantic Merge* with `UnmatchedEdge` (4 on the linear plan, 12 on the gallery bar). Task 4's regression test fails on that code already in *Semantic Merge*, with `Error UnmatchedEdge [Stair-2]: Edge (167.574, -143.864)-(170.691, -146.371) is neither shared with another zone nor on the plan boundary (gap or overlap).` and one more for `DwellingUnit-1`; with Task 3 applied it fails as Task 4, Step 3 shows (`FacadeNotCovered`), and with Task 4 it passes. `DisconnectedGroup` (Task 2) was observed only on the point plate, which is new in S8.2. The same probe on the replayed end state, with all eight families, gave `total 5632, failing 0`; the reference build reported `total 9408, failing 0` and `total 8960, failing 0` for its own sweeps, which were not re-run. ADR-006's new section (Task 11) says that the three faults "also affected the S8.1 bars and the linear plan"; what was observed on `v0.8.0` is the `UnmatchedEdge` fault, with the `FacadeNotCovered` fault behind it on the gallery bar. The plan keeps the reference text.
5. **The reverted alternative fix.** While the reference was built, a fix that restored the original vertices after every clipping operation (in the shared `PolygonOps.Execute`) was tried first; it changed two existing snapshots and was reverted, and it is in no reference commit (reference build report; not replayed). The adopted fix restores vertices only in `UnionTiles`, whose results are the outlines of united zones; `Intersect`, `Difference`, and `Union` keep their grid-rounded results, so overlap mapping, the perimeter/core shapes, and all twelve existing snapshots are unchanged.
6. **The small-zone clipping limit (not fixed).** Clipping rounds every vertex to the 1e-6 m grid (ADR-002, ADR-004), an absolute area error of the order of the perimeter times 1e-6 m, which for a zone of about 1 m² can exceed the relative area tolerance of 1e-6. Observed on the replayed end state: the point plate with `CoreWidth` 1 m (a 1 m² core, other parameters at their defaults), rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m, fails validation after *Semantic Merge*, *Perimeter Core*, and *Single Zone per Floor* with `FAIL Floor0.SourceCoverage: not fully mapped: ST` (*No Simplification* passes); in the probe of note 4 with 150 random placements, 6 of 154 placements fail every merging combination for that plate. The reference build reported the same limit at 124.1°, which was not reproduced at the three moves tried. S8.2 does not fix it: no tolerance may be widened (AGENTS.md rule 12), and a different treatment of small zones would change ADR-004 and needs its own decision. Every family's defaults validate at every tested placement. The plan commit adds a risk row to the roadmap.
7. **The `VertexGrid` tolerance is measured on unsnapped coordinates.** Before Task 3, `IsStrictlyInside` and `IsOnSegment` compared snapped grid cells: within 1 grid unit of the line, and more than half a unit from the ends. After it they compare the original point of each cell (the first point registered in it) in metres, against the grid's cell size, which is `ToleranceSettings.Distance` (`new VertexGrid(_tolerances.Distance)` and `new VertexGrid(_distance)`): within 1e-6 m of the line and more than 0.5e-6 m from the ends. The value is unchanged and no constant is added. Snapping a T-junction and both ends of its edge independently can move it up to √2 grid units off the snapped edge (1.10 units in Task 3's test), so the old test rejected vertices that lie exactly on the edge; the new one measures the true positions. On axis-aligned plans with vertices on the grid both tests agree, which is why every existing snapshot is unchanged.
8. **Zones with holes in S8.2.** D-084 assigns footprints with holes to S8.3. S8.2 already produces a zone with a hole: *Semantic Merge* unites the point plate's four dwellings into a ring around the core, as D-088 allows and as the types support since S2 (ADR-002). Task 8 validates that floor with every aggregator. Plan footprints with holes (the enclosed court) and *Perimeter Core* on them stay in S8.3.
9. ***Perimeter Core* and D-087.** The bisector rule of ADR-008 is unchanged; the numbered zones such as `P-South-1` come from its S3 rule for several façade runs in one direction. Task 7 changes only the two `PlanTooNarrow` messages; the precondition is not checked separately, because the existing checks (a valid inward offset that keeps every direction, trapezoids and core that tile the footprint) already reject such plans.
10. **Tasks without a red phase.** Task 8 adds verification tests only; it passed at once in the reference and in the replay, and the task says to stop if a case fails. Tasks 9 and 10 are Grasshopper code. The build-error red phases of Tasks 1, 5, and 6 list only declaration-level errors (four each): the compiler stops before binding method bodies.
11. **ADR-006 after the code.** Task 11 records the S8.2 details in ADR-006 after the code tasks, as the reference did. No record, default, or rule of ADR-006 changes, so the S8 gate (ADR-006 and ADR-011 accepted before any S8 code, satisfied in S8.1) is not affected.
12. **Icons.** The 32 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 10 (Pillow 12.3.0); the three new ones equal the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet description is from the replay's sheet.
13. **Smoke-test values.** The expected values of Task 14 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit: registration and inputs, the default plans (bounding boxes, the footprint line of *Inspect*, *Boundaries*, *Windows*, and the zone counts of three simplifiers), the 48 canonical and 96 rotated combinations (states and *Validate* Passed), the regression case, and five parameter scenarios. Unlike S8.1, the provenance texts were not recorded; the generator tests check them. *Inspect* texts beyond the footprint line, *Validate* values, *Convert2BEM* on the combinations, and the viewport were not observed. The reference assembly reported `0.5.0+<sha>` because the reference keeps the S5 version number.
14. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
15. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1, S4.2, and S8.1.
